## ---- 2. Functions -------------------------------------------------------------
# conditional demand after travel costs are multiplied by Fm (N x J matrix of factors),
# with income fixed and the marginal utility of income adjusting. For each camper, the new
# numeraire z solves  y - sum_j Fm_j p_j x_j(z) - z = 0, where
# x_j(z) = g_j [ (1 + x_j/g_j) / Fm_j (z / z0)^(1 - a1) - 1 ]  for visited sites (0 if negative).
# The left side falls in z, is positive at z = 0 and non-positive at z = y, so the root is
# bracketed and found by bisection; the budget residual is checked.
md_solve <- function(x, p, y, G, a1, Fm, iters = 200, return_z = FALSE) {
  z0 <- y - rowSums(p * x); stopifnot(all(z0 > 0))
  r <- 1 + x / G; vis <- x > 0
  dem <- function(z) ifelse(vis, pmax(0, G * (r / Fm * (z / z0)^(1 - a1) - 1)), 0)
  excess <- function(z) y - rowSums(Fm * p * dem(z)) - z
  lo <- rep(0, length(y)); hi <- y
  for (it in 1:iters) {
    mid <- (lo + hi) / 2; pos <- excess(mid) > 0
    lo[pos] <- mid[pos]; hi[!pos] <- mid[!pos]
  }
  zs <- (lo + hi) / 2
  if (max(abs(excess(zs))) > 1e-6) stop("md_solve: budget residual too large")
  if (return_z) list(x = dem(zs), z = zs) else dem(zs)
}

# elasticity of total trips when all travel costs rise by s (lambda adjusts)
md_elast <- function(x, p, y, g, a1, s) {
  v <- which(rowSums(x) > 0)
  G <- matrix(g, length(v), ncol(x), byrow = TRUE)
  xn <- md_solve(x[v, , drop = FALSE], p[v, , drop = FALSE], y[v], G, a1,
                 matrix(1 + s, length(v), ncol(x)))
  (sum(xn) / sum(x) - 1) / s
}

# baseline utility (numeraire weight normalised to 1)
md_U0 <- function(x, p, y, G, a1) {
  z <- y - rowSums(p * x)
  psi <- p * (1 + x / G) * z^(a1 - 1)
  list(U0 = rowSums(ifelse(x > 0, psi * G * log1p(x / G), 0)) + z^a1 / a1, psi = psi, z = z)
}

# conditional welfare loss of closing all sites (closed form)
md_welfare_all <- function(x, p, y, g, a1) {
  G <- matrix(g, nrow(x), ncol(x), byrow = TRUE)
  b <- md_U0(x, p, y, G, a1)
  sum((a1 * b$U0)^(1 / a1) - y)
}

# conditional (Hicksian) welfare loss of closing each site one at a time, holding sites not
# visited at baseline at zero trips (this can only raise the loss). For each camper who visited site k: find the numeraire z1 at which utility without
# site k (other visited sites re-optimised) equals baseline utility; the loss is the
# extra spending needed, z1 + sum p_j x_j(z1) - y.
md_welfare_sites <- function(x, p, y, g, a1, iters = 200, detail = FALSE) {
  N <- nrow(x); J <- ncol(x)
  G <- matrix(g, N, J, byrow = TRUE)
  b <- md_U0(x, p, y, G, a1)
  W <- numeric(J); det <- vector("list", J)
  for (k in 1:J) {
    idx <- which(x[, k] > 0)
    if (length(idx) == 0) next
    xs <- x[idx, , drop = FALSE]; ps <- p[idx, , drop = FALSE]; Gs <- G[idx, , drop = FALSE]
    psi <- b$psi[idx, , drop = FALSE]; U0 <- b$U0[idx]
    keep <- (xs > 0); keep[, k] <- FALSE                     # other visited sites
    util <- function(z) {                                     # utility at numeraire z (vector)
      lam <- z^(a1 - 1)
      ratio <- psi / (lam * ps)                               # = 1 + x/G at the optimum
      xj <- ifelse(keep & ratio > 1, Gs * (ratio - 1), 0)
      list(U = rowSums(ifelse(keep & ratio > 1, psi * Gs * log(ratio), 0)) + z^a1 / a1,
           spend = rowSums(ps * xj))
    }
    lo <- b$z[idx]                                            # utility too low here
    hi <- (a1 * U0)^(1 / a1)                                  # numeraire alone reaches U0
    for (it in 1:iters) {
      mid <- (lo + hi) / 2
      u <- util(mid)$U
      lo <- ifelse(u < U0, mid, lo); hi <- ifelse(u < U0, hi, mid)
    }
    z1 <- (lo + hi) / 2
    w_i <- z1 + util(z1)$spend - y[idx]
    W[k] <- sum(w_i)
    if (detail) det[[k]] <- list(idx = idx, z1 = z1, w = w_i)
  }
  if (detail) list(W = W, det = det, U0 = b$U0, R = ifelse(x > 0, b$psi, 0), z0 = b$z) else W
}

# compensating numeraire such that utility at prices P equals U0 (Hicksian), by bisection on
# log t = (1 - a1) log z between lt_lo (utility below U0) and lt_hi (utility above U0).
# Returns z1 and spending on sites at z1.
md_hicks_R <- function(R, P, U0, lt_lo, lt_hi, G, a1, iters = 200) {
  b <- 1 / (1 - a1); RP <- R / P; lRP <- ifelse(RP > 0, log(RP), -Inf)
  util <- function(lt) {
    on <- RP * exp(lt) > 1
    list(U = rowSums(ifelse(on, R * G * (lRP + lt), 0)) + exp(a1 * b * lt) / a1,
         spend = rowSums(ifelse(on, P * G * (RP * exp(lt) - 1), 0)))
  }
  lo <- lt_lo; hi <- lt_hi
  for (it in 1:iters) { mid <- (lo + hi) / 2; low <- util(mid)$U < U0; lo[low] <- mid[low]; hi[!low] <- mid[!low] }
  lt <- (lo + hi) / 2
  list(z1 = exp(b * lt), spend = util(lt)$spend)
}

# Modified Latin hypercube draws (Hess et al., 2006), as in rmdcev: for each of n vectors, D
# uniform draws, one in each interval [(d-1)/D, d/D), in random order. Returns an n x D matrix.
mlhs <- function(n, D) {
  U <- (matrix(rep(0:(D - 1), each = n), n, D) + matrix(runif(n * D), n, D)) / D
  idx <- t(apply(matrix(runif(n * D), n, D), 1, order))
  matrix(U[cbind(rep(1:n, D), as.vector(idx))], n, D)
}

# Conditional error draws, following rmdcev (Lloyd-Smith, 2021): the error of the numeraire is set
# to zero, the errors of visited sites follow from the first-order conditions at the observed trips,
# and the error of each site not visited is drawn from the type I extreme value distribution
# truncated so that the site is not chosen (R_j / p_j <= lambda). This gives
# R_j / (lambda p_j) = (1 + E / w_j)^(-sigma), with E = -log(u), u a modified Latin hypercube draw,
# and w_j = (psi_j / (lambda p_j))^(1/sigma). Sites without trips in the sample are not available.
# (numeraire = "posterior" instead draws the numeraire error from its distribution given the
# observed trips; this was used in an earlier version and is kept for reference.)
# Returns the visited R (N x J) and, for every draw, the unvisited R (N x J x D).
md_cond_draws <- function(x, p, y, g, a1, lpsi, scale, D, numeraire = c("zero", "posterior")) {
  numeraire <- match.arg(numeraire)
  N <- nrow(x); J <- ncol(x); G <- matrix(g, N, J, byrow = TRUE)
  vis <- x > 0; avail <- matrix(colSums(x) > 0, N, J, byrow = TRUE)
  z0 <- y - rowSums(p * x); lam0 <- z0^(a1 - 1)
  R <- ifelse(vis, p * (1 + x / G) * lam0, 0)
  psi <- matrix(exp(lpsi), N, J, byrow = TRUE)
  w <- (psi / (lam0 * p))^(1 / scale)
  unv <- !vis & avail
  K <- rowSums(vis)
  Rd <- array(0, c(N, J, D))
  if (numeraire == "zero") {
    U <- array(mlhs(N * J, D), c(N, J, D))                     # draws for camper i, site j in U[i, j, ]
    for (d in 1:D) Rd[, , d] <- ifelse(unv, lam0 * p * (1 - log(U[, , d]) / w)^(-scale), 0)
  } else {
    A <- 1 + rowSums(ifelse(vis, (psi / R)^(1 / scale), 0)) + rowSums(ifelse(unv, w, 0))
    for (d in 1:D) {
      t <- rgamma(N, shape = K + 1, rate = A)
      E <- matrix(rexp(N * J), N, J)
      Rd[, , d] <- ifelse(unv, lam0 * p * (1 + E / (t * w))^(-scale), 0)
    }
  }
  Rd[K == 0, , ] <- 0                       # campers without trips never start a trip after a cost rise
  list(R = R, Rd = Rd, G = G, z0 = z0, lam0 = lam0,
       Mx = apply(Rd / array(p, c(N, J, D)), c(1, 3), max))       # N x D: highest R_j/p_j among unvisited
}

# conditional welfare loss of closing each site, allowing unvisited sites to enter (same logic:
# the no-entry solution is kept unless an unvisited site would be chosen at its marginal utility
# of income). Returns the losses ($, all campers), the no-entry losses and the share with entry.
md_welfare_sites_cond <- function(CD, x, p, y, g, a1) {
  J <- ncol(x); D <- dim(CD$Rd)[3]
  ne <- md_welfare_sites(x, p, y, g, a1, detail = TRUE)
  W <- ne$W; share <- rep(NA_real_, J)
  for (k in 1:J) {
    d <- ne$det[[k]]; if (is.null(d)) next
    v <- d$idx; wk <- matrix(d$w, length(v), D)
    flag <- CD$Mx[v, , drop = FALSE] > d$z1^(a1 - 1)
    if (any(flag)) {
      fi <- which(flag, arr.ind = TRUE); ii <- v[fi[, 1]]
      Rf <- CD$R[ii, , drop = FALSE] + t(sapply(seq_len(nrow(fi)), function(m) CD$Rd[ii[m], , fi[m, 2]]))
      if (nrow(fi) == 1) Rf <- matrix(Rf, 1)
      Rf[, k] <- 0
      U0 <- ne$U0[ii]
      h <- md_hicks_R(Rf, p[ii, , drop = FALSE], U0, lt_lo = (1 - a1) * log(ne$z0[ii]),
                      lt_hi = (1 - a1) / a1 * log(a1 * U0), CD$G[ii, , drop = FALSE], a1)
      wk[fi] <- h$z1 + h$spend - y[ii]
    }
    W[k] <- sum(wk) / D; share[k] <- mean(flag)
  }
  list(W = W, W_ne = ne$W, share = share)
}

## ---- 3. Data in the order rmdcev uses ----------------------------------------
sd <- mdcev_mle$stan_data
x  <- sd$quant_j; p <- sd$price_j; y <- as.numeric(sd$income)
N  <- sd$I; J <- sd$J
sites <- as.character(data_model$parkname[data_model$id == data_model$id[1]])
stopifnot(length(sites) == J)

## parameters from one row of draws (or the point estimates): site 1 is the base (psi = 0)
get_pars <- function(th) {
  nm <- names(th)
  list(lpsi  = c(0, th[grepl("^psi\\[", nm)]),
       g     = th[grepl("^gamma\\[", nm)],
       a1    = th[grepl("^alpha\\[", nm)][1],
       scale = th[grepl("^scale\\[", nm)][1])
}

## welfare (per person) and total-trip elasticities for one parameter set
md_measures <- function(pr, seed) {
  set.seed(seed)                                                    # MLHS draws for unvisited sites
  CD <- md_cond_draws(x, p, y, pr$g, pr$a1, pr$lpsi, pr$scale, NERRS)
  W  <- c(md_welfare_sites_cond(CD, x, p, y, pr$g, pr$a1)$W,
          md_welfare_all(x, p, y, pr$g, pr$a1)) / N
  el <- sapply(s_vec, function(s) md_elast(x, p, y, pr$g, pr$a1, s))
  c(-W, el)                                                         # losses negative, as rmdcev
}
lab <- c(sites, "System closure", paste0("el +", 100 * s_vec, "%"))

## ---- 4. Point estimates ---------------------------------------------------------
pt <- unlist(mdcev_mle$stan_fit$par[c("psi", "gamma", "alpha", "scale")])
names(pt) <- c(paste0("psi[1,", seq_len(J - 1), "]"), paste0("gamma[1,", seq_len(J), "]"),
               "alpha[1,1]", "scale[1]")
res_point <- setNames(md_measures(get_pars(pt), seed = 1), lab)

## ---- 5. The same 100 parameter draws as PrepareSimulationData -------------------

## The manual script re-estimates mdcev_mle; use the stored rmdcev model if it exists.
mod_rm <- if (exists("mdcev_model_list") && !is.null(mdcev_model_list[["2018"]]))
            mdcev_model_list[["2018"]] else mdcev_mle
th <- mod_rm$stan_fit$theta_tilde

par_names <- c("psi", "gamma", "alpha", "scale")
if (!isTRUE(all.equal(unlist(mod_rm$stan_fit$par[par_names]),
                      unlist(mdcev_mle$stan_fit$par[par_names]))))
  warning("point estimates differ between the rmdcev run and the manual re-estimation")

## ---- 1. rmdcev summary, put back in numeric policy order ------------------------------
## rmdcev sorts policy names as text (policy1, policy10, ..., policy2, ...).
rm_tab  <- as.data.frame(summary(welfare_2018)$CoefTable)
pol_col <- names(rm_tab)[!sapply(rm_tab, is.numeric)][1]
pol_num <- as.integer(gsub("\\D", "", rm_tab[[pol_col]]))
stopifnot(identical(sort(pol_num), 1:(J + 1)))
rm_tab  <- rm_tab[order(pol_num), ]

num  <- rm_tab[sapply(rm_tab, is.numeric)]
pick <- function(pattern) num[[grep(pattern, names(num), ignore.case = TRUE)[1]]]
rm_mean <- pick("mean"); rm_lo <- pick("lo"); rm_hi <- pick("hi")

## ---- 2. rmdcev welfare for each parameter draw ----------------------------------------
## welfare_2018: list of N per-person matrices, parameter draws x policies
stopifnot(is.list(welfare_2018), length(welfare_2018) == N)
rm_draws <- Reduce(`+`, lapply(welfare_2018, function(m) as.matrix(m)[, 1:(J + 1)])) / N
cat("rmdcev parameter draws:", nrow(rm_draws), "\n")
rmdcev parameter draws: 30 
## ---- 3. Identify which rows of theta_tilde rmdcev used ---------------------------------
## System-closure welfare depends only on gamma and alpha and has a closed form, so compute
## it for every row of theta_tilde and find the row that reproduces each rmdcev draw.
sys_all <- apply(th, 1, function(r) {
  pr <- get_pars(r); -md_welfare_all(x, p, y, pr$g, pr$a1) / N
})
rm_sys <- rm_draws[, J + 1]
gap <- sapply(rm_sys, function(v) min(abs(v - sys_all)))
idx <- sapply(rm_sys, function(v) which.min(abs(v - sys_all)))
cat("rmdcev draws reproduced by a theta_tilde row:", sum(gap < 1e-4), "of", length(rm_sys),
    "(largest gap", signif(max(gap), 3), ")\n")
rmdcev draws reproduced by a theta_tilde row: 30 of 30 (largest gap 1.66e-10 )
stopifnot(all(gap < 1e-4))

## ---- 4. Manual welfare on exactly those draws -----------------------------------------
welf_one <- function(pr, seed) {
  set.seed(seed)                                              # MLHS draws for unvisited sites
  CD  <- md_cond_draws(x, p, y, pr$g, pr$a1, pr$lpsi, pr$scale, NERRS)
  out <- md_welfare_sites_cond(CD, x, p, y, pr$g, pr$a1)
  list(W = -c(out$W, md_welfare_all(x, p, y, pr$g, pr$a1)) / N, share = out$share)
}

t0  <- Sys.time()
res <- lapply(seq_along(idx), function(r) welf_one(get_pars(th[idx[r], ]), seed = 1000 + r))
cat("time for", length(idx), "draws:", format(Sys.time() - t0), "\n")
time for 30 draws: 33.95512 secs 
man_draws <- t(sapply(res, `[[`, "W"))
entry     <- t(sapply(res, `[[`, "share"))              # share of camper-draws switching to a new site
colnames(man_draws) <- colnames(rm_draws) <- c(sites, "System closure")
colnames(entry) <- sites

## ---- 5. Compare -----------------------------------------------------------------------
man_mean <- colMeans(man_draws)
cmp_same <- data.frame(
  measure       = c(sites, "System closure"),

  pct_diff      = 100 * (man_mean - rm_mean) / abs(rm_mean),

  man_ci        = sprintf("[%.2f, %.2f]",
                          apply(man_draws, 2, quantile, 0.025),
                          apply(man_draws, 2, quantile, 0.975))
)

Sensitivity

## ---- 6. Sensitivity to the satiation parameters: gamma x 2 and gamma x 4 ------
## Paste at the end of fast_mdcev_YEAR.R (uses th, idx, get_pars, md_measures, lab, YEAR).
## All gammas are scaled by the same factor; the other parameters stay at their draws.
## The conditional errors are recomputed, so each scaled model still fits the observed trips.
## Note: a larger gamma means LESS satiation (trips lose value more slowly).
g_scale <- c(2, 4)
scaled <- lapply(g_scale, function(f) {
  Mf <- t(sapply(seq_len(NSIMS), function(r) {
    pr <- get_pars(th[idx[r], ]); pr$g <- pr$g * f
    md_measures(pr, seed = 1000 + r)
  }))
  colnames(Mf) <- lab
  data.frame(measure = lab, gamma_x = f, mean = colMeans(Mf),
             ci_lo = apply(Mf, 2, quantile, 0.025), ci_hi = apply(Mf, 2, quantile, 0.975),
             row.names = NULL)
})
base <- get(paste0("fast_", YEAR))                     # original gammas (section 5)
gamma_scaling <- rbind(data.frame(measure = base$measure, gamma_x = 1, mean = base$mean,
                                  ci_lo = base$ci_lo, ci_hi = base$ci_hi),
                       do.call(rbind, scaled))
## side by side: original, x2, x4 (means)
print(reshape(gamma_scaling[, c("measure", "gamma_x", "mean")], idvar = "measure",
              timevar = "gamma_x", direction = "wide"), digits = 4, row.names = FALSE)

2019

################
# 2019 - MDCEV #
################

## Fast MDCEV welfare and total-trip elasticities, 2019 (gamma profile)
## Same model and the same 100 parameter draws as the rmdcev code, but welfare
## and elasticities are computed directly instead of with mdcev.sim:
##  - conditional errors as in rmdcev (numeraire error zero, visited sites from the
##    first-order conditions, unvisited sites from truncated draws, 100 MLHS draws)
##  - welfare: compensating variation of closing each site one at a time and
##    closing all sites (losses per person, negative as in rmdcev's summary)
##  - total-trip elasticity: all travel costs +1, 10, 20, 50 percent, with the
##    marginal utility of income adjusting so the budget holds
## Requires final_data_2019 in memory.
##########################################################################

YEAR  <- 2019
s_vec <- c(0.01, 0.10, 0.20, 0.50)

## ---- 1. Estimate (identical to the rmdcev code) ------------------------------
data_model <- mdcev.data(final_data_2019, id.var = "id", alt.var = "parkname", choice = "VisitCount")
mdcev_mle  <- mdcev(~ 1, data = data_model, model = "gamma", algorithm = "MLE",
                    std_errors = "mvn", n_draws = 100, trunc_data = 1, print_iterations = FALSE)

## ---- 2. Functions -------------------------------------------------------------
# conditional demand after travel costs are multiplied by Fm (N x J matrix of factors),
# with income fixed and the marginal utility of income adjusting. For each camper, the new
# numeraire z solves  y - sum_j Fm_j p_j x_j(z) - z = 0, where
# x_j(z) = g_j [ (1 + x_j/g_j) / Fm_j (z / z0)^(1 - a1) - 1 ]  for visited sites (0 if negative).
# The left side falls in z, is positive at z = 0 and non-positive at z = y, so the root is
# bracketed and found by bisection; the budget residual is checked.
md_solve <- function(x, p, y, G, a1, Fm, iters = 200, return_z = FALSE) {
  z0 <- y - rowSums(p * x); stopifnot(all(z0 > 0))
  r <- 1 + x / G; vis <- x > 0
  dem <- function(z) ifelse(vis, pmax(0, G * (r / Fm * (z / z0)^(1 - a1) - 1)), 0)
  excess <- function(z) y - rowSums(Fm * p * dem(z)) - z
  lo <- rep(0, length(y)); hi <- y
  for (it in 1:iters) {
    mid <- (lo + hi) / 2; pos <- excess(mid) > 0
    lo[pos] <- mid[pos]; hi[!pos] <- mid[!pos]
  }
  zs <- (lo + hi) / 2
  if (max(abs(excess(zs))) > 1e-6) stop("md_solve: budget residual too large")
  if (return_z) list(x = dem(zs), z = zs) else dem(zs)
}


# elasticity of total trips when all travel costs rise by s (lambda adjusts)
md_elast <- function(x, p, y, g, a1, s) {
  v <- which(rowSums(x) > 0)
  G <- matrix(g, length(v), ncol(x), byrow = TRUE)
  xn <- md_solve(x[v, , drop = FALSE], p[v, , drop = FALSE], y[v], G, a1,
                 matrix(1 + s, length(v), ncol(x)))
  (sum(xn) / sum(x) - 1) / s
}

# baseline utility (numeraire weight normalised to 1)
md_U0 <- function(x, p, y, G, a1) {
  z <- y - rowSums(p * x)
  psi <- p * (1 + x / G) * z^(a1 - 1)
  list(U0 = rowSums(ifelse(x > 0, psi * G * log1p(x / G), 0)) + z^a1 / a1, psi = psi, z = z)
}

# conditional welfare loss of closing all sites (closed form)
md_welfare_all <- function(x, p, y, g, a1) {
  G <- matrix(g, nrow(x), ncol(x), byrow = TRUE)
  b <- md_U0(x, p, y, G, a1)
  sum((a1 * b$U0)^(1 / a1) - y)
}

# conditional (Hicksian) welfare loss of closing each site one at a time, holding sites not
# visited at baseline at zero trips (this can only raise the loss). For each camper who visited site k: find the numeraire z1 at which utility without
# site k (other visited sites re-optimised) equals baseline utility; the loss is the
# extra spending needed, z1 + sum p_j x_j(z1) - y.
md_welfare_sites <- function(x, p, y, g, a1, iters = 200, detail = FALSE) {
  N <- nrow(x); J <- ncol(x)
  G <- matrix(g, N, J, byrow = TRUE)
  b <- md_U0(x, p, y, G, a1)
  W <- numeric(J); det <- vector("list", J)
  for (k in 1:J) {
    idx <- which(x[, k] > 0)
    if (length(idx) == 0) next
    xs <- x[idx, , drop = FALSE]; ps <- p[idx, , drop = FALSE]; Gs <- G[idx, , drop = FALSE]
    psi <- b$psi[idx, , drop = FALSE]; U0 <- b$U0[idx]
    keep <- (xs > 0); keep[, k] <- FALSE                     # other visited sites
    util <- function(z) {                                     # utility at numeraire z (vector)
      lam <- z^(a1 - 1)
      ratio <- psi / (lam * ps)                               # = 1 + x/G at the optimum
      xj <- ifelse(keep & ratio > 1, Gs * (ratio - 1), 0)
      list(U = rowSums(ifelse(keep & ratio > 1, psi * Gs * log(ratio), 0)) + z^a1 / a1,
           spend = rowSums(ps * xj))
    }
    lo <- b$z[idx]                                            # utility too low here
    hi <- (a1 * U0)^(1 / a1)                                  # numeraire alone reaches U0
    for (it in 1:iters) {
      mid <- (lo + hi) / 2
      u <- util(mid)$U
      lo <- ifelse(u < U0, mid, lo); hi <- ifelse(u < U0, hi, mid)
    }
    z1 <- (lo + hi) / 2
    w_i <- z1 + util(z1)$spend - y[idx]
    W[k] <- sum(w_i)
    if (detail) det[[k]] <- list(idx = idx, z1 = z1, w = w_i)
  }
  if (detail) list(W = W, det = det, U0 = b$U0, R = ifelse(x > 0, b$psi, 0), z0 = b$z) else W
}

# compensating numeraire such that utility at prices P equals U0 (Hicksian), by bisection on
# log t = (1 - a1) log z between lt_lo (utility below U0) and lt_hi (utility above U0).
# Returns z1 and spending on sites at z1.
md_hicks_R <- function(R, P, U0, lt_lo, lt_hi, G, a1, iters = 200) {
  b <- 1 / (1 - a1); RP <- R / P; lRP <- ifelse(RP > 0, log(RP), -Inf)
  util <- function(lt) {
    on <- RP * exp(lt) > 1
    list(U = rowSums(ifelse(on, R * G * (lRP + lt), 0)) + exp(a1 * b * lt) / a1,
         spend = rowSums(ifelse(on, P * G * (RP * exp(lt) - 1), 0)))
  }
  lo <- lt_lo; hi <- lt_hi
  for (it in 1:iters) { mid <- (lo + hi) / 2; low <- util(mid)$U < U0; lo[low] <- mid[low]; hi[!low] <- mid[!low] }
  lt <- (lo + hi) / 2
  list(z1 = exp(b * lt), spend = util(lt)$spend)
}

# Modified Latin hypercube draws (Hess et al., 2006), as in rmdcev: for each of n vectors, D
# uniform draws, one in each interval [(d-1)/D, d/D), in random order. Returns an n x D matrix.
mlhs <- function(n, D) {
  U <- (matrix(rep(0:(D - 1), each = n), n, D) + matrix(runif(n * D), n, D)) / D
  idx <- t(apply(matrix(runif(n * D), n, D), 1, order))
  matrix(U[cbind(rep(1:n, D), as.vector(idx))], n, D)
}

# Conditional error draws, following rmdcev (Lloyd-Smith, 2021): the error of the numeraire is set
# to zero, the errors of visited sites follow from the first-order conditions at the observed trips,
# and the error of each site not visited is drawn from the type I extreme value distribution
# truncated so that the site is not chosen (R_j / p_j <= lambda). This gives
# R_j / (lambda p_j) = (1 + E / w_j)^(-sigma), with E = -log(u), u a modified Latin hypercube draw,
# and w_j = (psi_j / (lambda p_j))^(1/sigma). Sites without trips in the sample are not available.
# (numeraire = "posterior" instead draws the numeraire error from its distribution given the
# observed trips; this was used in an earlier version and is kept for reference.)
# Returns the visited R (N x J) and, for every draw, the unvisited R (N x J x D).
md_cond_draws <- function(x, p, y, g, a1, lpsi, scale, D, numeraire = c("zero", "posterior")) {
  numeraire <- match.arg(numeraire)
  N <- nrow(x); J <- ncol(x); G <- matrix(g, N, J, byrow = TRUE)
  vis <- x > 0; avail <- matrix(colSums(x) > 0, N, J, byrow = TRUE)
  z0 <- y - rowSums(p * x); lam0 <- z0^(a1 - 1)
  R <- ifelse(vis, p * (1 + x / G) * lam0, 0)
  psi <- matrix(exp(lpsi), N, J, byrow = TRUE)
  w <- (psi / (lam0 * p))^(1 / scale)
  unv <- !vis & avail
  K <- rowSums(vis)
  Rd <- array(0, c(N, J, D))
  if (numeraire == "zero") {
    U <- array(mlhs(N * J, D), c(N, J, D))                     # draws for camper i, site j in U[i, j, ]
    for (d in 1:D) Rd[, , d] <- ifelse(unv, lam0 * p * (1 - log(U[, , d]) / w)^(-scale), 0)
  } else {
    A <- 1 + rowSums(ifelse(vis, (psi / R)^(1 / scale), 0)) + rowSums(ifelse(unv, w, 0))
    for (d in 1:D) {
      t <- rgamma(N, shape = K + 1, rate = A)
      E <- matrix(rexp(N * J), N, J)
      Rd[, , d] <- ifelse(unv, lam0 * p * (1 + E / (t * w))^(-scale), 0)
    }
  }
  Rd[K == 0, , ] <- 0                       # campers without trips never start a trip after a cost rise
  list(R = R, Rd = Rd, G = G, z0 = z0, lam0 = lam0,
       Mx = apply(Rd / array(p, c(N, J, D)), c(1, 3), max))       # N x D: highest R_j/p_j among unvisited
}

# conditional welfare loss of closing each site, allowing unvisited sites to enter (same logic:
# the no-entry solution is kept unless an unvisited site would be chosen at its marginal utility
# of income). Returns the losses ($, all campers), the no-entry losses and the share with entry.
md_welfare_sites_cond <- function(CD, x, p, y, g, a1) {
  J <- ncol(x); D <- dim(CD$Rd)[3]
  ne <- md_welfare_sites(x, p, y, g, a1, detail = TRUE)
  W <- ne$W; share <- rep(NA_real_, J)
  for (k in 1:J) {
    d <- ne$det[[k]]; if (is.null(d)) next
    v <- d$idx; wk <- matrix(d$w, length(v), D)
    flag <- CD$Mx[v, , drop = FALSE] > d$z1^(a1 - 1)
    if (any(flag)) {
      fi <- which(flag, arr.ind = TRUE); ii <- v[fi[, 1]]
      Rf <- CD$R[ii, , drop = FALSE] + t(sapply(seq_len(nrow(fi)), function(m) CD$Rd[ii[m], , fi[m, 2]]))
      if (nrow(fi) == 1) Rf <- matrix(Rf, 1)
      Rf[, k] <- 0
      U0 <- ne$U0[ii]
      h <- md_hicks_R(Rf, p[ii, , drop = FALSE], U0, lt_lo = (1 - a1) * log(ne$z0[ii]),
                      lt_hi = (1 - a1) / a1 * log(a1 * U0), CD$G[ii, , drop = FALSE], a1)
      wk[fi] <- h$z1 + h$spend - y[ii]
    }
    W[k] <- sum(wk) / D; share[k] <- mean(flag)
  }
  list(W = W, W_ne = ne$W, share = share)
}

## ---- 3. Data in the order rmdcev uses ----------------------------------------
sd <- mdcev_mle$stan_data
x  <- sd$quant_j; p <- sd$price_j; y <- as.numeric(sd$income)
N  <- sd$I; J <- sd$J
sites <- as.character(data_model$parkname[data_model$id == data_model$id[1]])
stopifnot(length(sites) == J)

## parameters from one row of draws (or the point estimates): site 1 is the base (psi = 0)
get_pars <- function(th) {
  nm <- names(th)
  list(lpsi  = c(0, th[grepl("^psi\\[", nm)]),
       g     = th[grepl("^gamma\\[", nm)],
       a1    = th[grepl("^alpha\\[", nm)][1],
       scale = th[grepl("^scale\\[", nm)][1])
}

## welfare (per person) and total-trip elasticities for one parameter set
md_measures <- function(pr, seed) {
  set.seed(seed)                                                    # MLHS draws for unvisited sites
  CD <- md_cond_draws(x, p, y, pr$g, pr$a1, pr$lpsi, pr$scale, NERRS)
  W  <- c(md_welfare_sites_cond(CD, x, p, y, pr$g, pr$a1)$W,
          md_welfare_all(x, p, y, pr$g, pr$a1)) / N
  el <- sapply(s_vec, function(s) md_elast(x, p, y, pr$g, pr$a1, s))
  c(-W, el)                                                         # losses negative, as rmdcev
}
lab <- c(sites, "System closure", paste0("el +", 100 * s_vec, "%"))

names(pt) <- c(paste0("psi[1,", seq_len(J - 1), "]"), paste0("gamma[1,", seq_len(J), "]"),
               "alpha[1,1]", "scale[1]")
res_point <- setNames(md_measures(get_pars(pt), seed = 1), lab)

## ---- 6. Sensitivity to the satiation parameters: gamma x 2 and gamma x 4 ------
## Paste at the end of fast_mdcev_YEAR.R (uses th, idx, get_pars, md_measures, lab, YEAR).
## All gammas are scaled by the same factor; the other parameters stay at their draws.
## The conditional errors are recomputed, so each scaled model still fits the observed trips.
## Note: a larger gamma means LESS satiation (trips lose value more slowly).
g_scale <- c(2, 4)
scaled <- lapply(g_scale, function(f) {
  Mf <- t(sapply(seq_len(NSIMS), function(r) {
    pr <- get_pars(th[idx[r], ]); pr$g <- pr$g * f
    md_measures(pr, seed = 1000 + r)
  }))
  colnames(Mf) <- lab
  data.frame(measure = lab, gamma_x = f, mean = colMeans(Mf),
             ci_lo = apply(Mf, 2, quantile, 0.025), ci_hi = apply(Mf, 2, quantile, 0.975),
             row.names = NULL)
})
base <- get(paste0("fast_", YEAR))                     # original gammas (section 5)
gamma_scaling <- rbind(data.frame(measure = base$measure, gamma_x = 1, mean = base$mean,
                                  ci_lo = base$ci_lo, ci_hi = base$ci_hi),
                       do.call(rbind, scaled))
## side by side: original, x2, x4 (means)

##########################################################################
## Fast MDCEV welfare and total-trip elasticities, 2020 (gamma profile)
## Same model and the same 100 parameter draws as the rmdcev code, but welfare
## and elasticities are computed directly instead of with mdcev.sim:
##  - conditional errors as in rmdcev (numeraire error zero, visited sites from the
##    first-order conditions, unvisited sites from truncated draws, 100 MLHS draws)
##  - welfare: compensating variation of closing each site one at a time and
##    closing all sites (losses per person, negative as in rmdcev's summary)
##  - total-trip elasticity: all travel costs +1, 10, 20, 50 percent, with the
##    marginal utility of income adjusting so the budget holds
## Requires final_data_2019 in memory.
##########################################################################
suppressMessages({ library(rmdcev); library(dplyr) })
YEAR  <- 2020
s_vec <- c(0.01, 0.10, 0.20, 0.50)

## ---- 1. Estimate (identical to the rmdcev code) ------------------------------
data_model <- mdcev.data(final_data_2020, id.var = "id", alt.var = "parkname", choice = "VisitCount")
mdcev_mle  <- mdcev(~ 1, data = data_model, model = "gamma", algorithm = "MLE",
                    std_errors = "mvn", n_draws = 100, trunc_data = 1, print_iterations = FALSE)

## ---- 2. Functions -------------------------------------------------------------
# conditional demand after travel costs are multiplied by Fm (N x J matrix of factors),
# with income fixed and the marginal utility of income adjusting. For each camper, the new
# numeraire z solves  y - sum_j Fm_j p_j x_j(z) - z = 0, where
# x_j(z) = g_j [ (1 + x_j/g_j) / Fm_j (z / z0)^(1 - a1) - 1 ]  for visited sites (0 if negative).
# The left side falls in z, is positive at z = 0 and non-positive at z = y, so the root is
# bracketed and found by bisection; the budget residual is checked.
md_solve <- function(x, p, y, G, a1, Fm, iters = 200, return_z = FALSE) {
  z0 <- y - rowSums(p * x); stopifnot(all(z0 > 0))
  r <- 1 + x / G; vis <- x > 0
  dem <- function(z) ifelse(vis, pmax(0, G * (r / Fm * (z / z0)^(1 - a1) - 1)), 0)
  excess <- function(z) y - rowSums(Fm * p * dem(z)) - z
  lo <- rep(0, length(y)); hi <- y
  for (it in 1:iters) {
    mid <- (lo + hi) / 2; pos <- excess(mid) > 0
    lo[pos] <- mid[pos]; hi[!pos] <- mid[!pos]
  }
  zs <- (lo + hi) / 2
  if (max(abs(excess(zs))) > 1e-6) stop("md_solve: budget residual too large")
  if (return_z) list(x = dem(zs), z = zs) else dem(zs)
}


# elasticity of total trips when all travel costs rise by s (lambda adjusts)
md_elast <- function(x, p, y, g, a1, s) {
  v <- which(rowSums(x) > 0)
  G <- matrix(g, length(v), ncol(x), byrow = TRUE)
  xn <- md_solve(x[v, , drop = FALSE], p[v, , drop = FALSE], y[v], G, a1,
                 matrix(1 + s, length(v), ncol(x)))
  (sum(xn) / sum(x) - 1) / s
}

# baseline utility (numeraire weight normalised to 1)
md_U0 <- function(x, p, y, G, a1) {
  z <- y - rowSums(p * x)
  psi <- p * (1 + x / G) * z^(a1 - 1)
  list(U0 = rowSums(ifelse(x > 0, psi * G * log1p(x / G), 0)) + z^a1 / a1, psi = psi, z = z)
}

# conditional welfare loss of closing all sites (closed form)
md_welfare_all <- function(x, p, y, g, a1) {
  G <- matrix(g, nrow(x), ncol(x), byrow = TRUE)
  b <- md_U0(x, p, y, G, a1)
  sum((a1 * b$U0)^(1 / a1) - y)
}

# conditional (Hicksian) welfare loss of closing each site one at a time, holding sites not
# visited at baseline at zero trips (this can only raise the loss). For each camper who visited site k: find the numeraire z1 at which utility without
# site k (other visited sites re-optimised) equals baseline utility; the loss is the
# extra spending needed, z1 + sum p_j x_j(z1) - y.
md_welfare_sites <- function(x, p, y, g, a1, iters = 200, detail = FALSE) {
  N <- nrow(x); J <- ncol(x)
  G <- matrix(g, N, J, byrow = TRUE)
  b <- md_U0(x, p, y, G, a1)
  W <- numeric(J); det <- vector("list", J)
  for (k in 1:J) {
    idx <- which(x[, k] > 0)
    if (length(idx) == 0) next
    xs <- x[idx, , drop = FALSE]; ps <- p[idx, , drop = FALSE]; Gs <- G[idx, , drop = FALSE]
    psi <- b$psi[idx, , drop = FALSE]; U0 <- b$U0[idx]
    keep <- (xs > 0); keep[, k] <- FALSE                     # other visited sites
    util <- function(z) {                                     # utility at numeraire z (vector)
      lam <- z^(a1 - 1)
      ratio <- psi / (lam * ps)                               # = 1 + x/G at the optimum
      xj <- ifelse(keep & ratio > 1, Gs * (ratio - 1), 0)
      list(U = rowSums(ifelse(keep & ratio > 1, psi * Gs * log(ratio), 0)) + z^a1 / a1,
           spend = rowSums(ps * xj))
    }
    lo <- b$z[idx]                                            # utility too low here
    hi <- (a1 * U0)^(1 / a1)                                  # numeraire alone reaches U0
    for (it in 1:iters) {
      mid <- (lo + hi) / 2
      u <- util(mid)$U
      lo <- ifelse(u < U0, mid, lo); hi <- ifelse(u < U0, hi, mid)
    }
    z1 <- (lo + hi) / 2
    w_i <- z1 + util(z1)$spend - y[idx]
    W[k] <- sum(w_i)
    if (detail) det[[k]] <- list(idx = idx, z1 = z1, w = w_i)
  }
  if (detail) list(W = W, det = det, U0 = b$U0, R = ifelse(x > 0, b$psi, 0), z0 = b$z) else W
}

# compensating numeraire such that utility at prices P equals U0 (Hicksian), by bisection on
# log t = (1 - a1) log z between lt_lo (utility below U0) and lt_hi (utility above U0).
# Returns z1 and spending on sites at z1.
md_hicks_R <- function(R, P, U0, lt_lo, lt_hi, G, a1, iters = 200) {
  b <- 1 / (1 - a1); RP <- R / P; lRP <- ifelse(RP > 0, log(RP), -Inf)
  util <- function(lt) {
    on <- RP * exp(lt) > 1
    list(U = rowSums(ifelse(on, R * G * (lRP + lt), 0)) + exp(a1 * b * lt) / a1,
         spend = rowSums(ifelse(on, P * G * (RP * exp(lt) - 1), 0)))
  }
  lo <- lt_lo; hi <- lt_hi
  for (it in 1:iters) { mid <- (lo + hi) / 2; low <- util(mid)$U < U0; lo[low] <- mid[low]; hi[!low] <- mid[!low] }
  lt <- (lo + hi) / 2
  list(z1 = exp(b * lt), spend = util(lt)$spend)
}

# Modified Latin hypercube draws (Hess et al., 2006), as in rmdcev: for each of n vectors, D
# uniform draws, one in each interval [(d-1)/D, d/D), in random order. Returns an n x D matrix.
mlhs <- function(n, D) {
  U <- (matrix(rep(0:(D - 1), each = n), n, D) + matrix(runif(n * D), n, D)) / D
  idx <- t(apply(matrix(runif(n * D), n, D), 1, order))
  matrix(U[cbind(rep(1:n, D), as.vector(idx))], n, D)
}

# Conditional error draws, following rmdcev (Lloyd-Smith, 2021): the error of the numeraire is set
# to zero, the errors of visited sites follow from the first-order conditions at the observed trips,
# and the error of each site not visited is drawn from the type I extreme value distribution
# truncated so that the site is not chosen (R_j / p_j <= lambda). This gives
# R_j / (lambda p_j) = (1 + E / w_j)^(-sigma), with E = -log(u), u a modified Latin hypercube draw,
# and w_j = (psi_j / (lambda p_j))^(1/sigma). Sites without trips in the sample are not available.
# (numeraire = "posterior" instead draws the numeraire error from its distribution given the
# observed trips; this was used in an earlier version and is kept for reference.)
# Returns the visited R (N x J) and, for every draw, the unvisited R (N x J x D).
md_cond_draws <- function(x, p, y, g, a1, lpsi, scale, D, numeraire = c("zero", "posterior")) {
  numeraire <- match.arg(numeraire)
  N <- nrow(x); J <- ncol(x); G <- matrix(g, N, J, byrow = TRUE)
  vis <- x > 0; avail <- matrix(colSums(x) > 0, N, J, byrow = TRUE)
  z0 <- y - rowSums(p * x); lam0 <- z0^(a1 - 1)
  R <- ifelse(vis, p * (1 + x / G) * lam0, 0)
  psi <- matrix(exp(lpsi), N, J, byrow = TRUE)
  w <- (psi / (lam0 * p))^(1 / scale)
  unv <- !vis & avail
  K <- rowSums(vis)
  Rd <- array(0, c(N, J, D))
  if (numeraire == "zero") {
    U <- array(mlhs(N * J, D), c(N, J, D))                     # draws for camper i, site j in U[i, j, ]
    for (d in 1:D) Rd[, , d] <- ifelse(unv, lam0 * p * (1 - log(U[, , d]) / w)^(-scale), 0)
  } else {
    A <- 1 + rowSums(ifelse(vis, (psi / R)^(1 / scale), 0)) + rowSums(ifelse(unv, w, 0))
    for (d in 1:D) {
      t <- rgamma(N, shape = K + 1, rate = A)
      E <- matrix(rexp(N * J), N, J)
      Rd[, , d] <- ifelse(unv, lam0 * p * (1 + E / (t * w))^(-scale), 0)
    }
  }
  Rd[K == 0, , ] <- 0                       # campers without trips never start a trip after a cost rise
  list(R = R, Rd = Rd, G = G, z0 = z0, lam0 = lam0,
       Mx = apply(Rd / array(p, c(N, J, D)), c(1, 3), max))       # N x D: highest R_j/p_j among unvisited
}

# conditional welfare loss of closing each site, allowing unvisited sites to enter (same logic:
# the no-entry solution is kept unless an unvisited site would be chosen at its marginal utility
# of income). Returns the losses ($, all campers), the no-entry losses and the share with entry.
md_welfare_sites_cond <- function(CD, x, p, y, g, a1) {
  J <- ncol(x); D <- dim(CD$Rd)[3]
  ne <- md_welfare_sites(x, p, y, g, a1, detail = TRUE)
  W <- ne$W; share <- rep(NA_real_, J)
  for (k in 1:J) {
    d <- ne$det[[k]]; if (is.null(d)) next
    v <- d$idx; wk <- matrix(d$w, length(v), D)
    flag <- CD$Mx[v, , drop = FALSE] > d$z1^(a1 - 1)
    if (any(flag)) {
      fi <- which(flag, arr.ind = TRUE); ii <- v[fi[, 1]]
      Rf <- CD$R[ii, , drop = FALSE] + t(sapply(seq_len(nrow(fi)), function(m) CD$Rd[ii[m], , fi[m, 2]]))
      if (nrow(fi) == 1) Rf <- matrix(Rf, 1)
      Rf[, k] <- 0
      U0 <- ne$U0[ii]
      h <- md_hicks_R(Rf, p[ii, , drop = FALSE], U0, lt_lo = (1 - a1) * log(ne$z0[ii]),
                      lt_hi = (1 - a1) / a1 * log(a1 * U0), CD$G[ii, , drop = FALSE], a1)
      wk[fi] <- h$z1 + h$spend - y[ii]
    }
    W[k] <- sum(wk) / D; share[k] <- mean(flag)
  }
  list(W = W, W_ne = ne$W, share = share)
}

## ---- 3. Data in the order rmdcev uses ----------------------------------------
sd <- mdcev_mle$stan_data
x  <- sd$quant_j; p <- sd$price_j; y <- as.numeric(sd$income)
N  <- sd$I; J <- sd$J
sites <- as.character(data_model$parkname[data_model$id == data_model$id[1]])
stopifnot(length(sites) == J)

## parameters from one row of draws (or the point estimates): site 1 is the base (psi = 0)
get_pars <- function(th) {
  nm <- names(th)
  list(lpsi  = c(0, th[grepl("^psi\\[", nm)]),
       g     = th[grepl("^gamma\\[", nm)],
       a1    = th[grepl("^alpha\\[", nm)][1],
       scale = th[grepl("^scale\\[", nm)][1])
}

## welfare (per person) and total-trip elasticities for one parameter set
md_measures <- function(pr, seed) {
  set.seed(seed)                                                    # MLHS draws for unvisited sites
  CD <- md_cond_draws(x, p, y, pr$g, pr$a1, pr$lpsi, pr$scale, NERRS)
  W  <- c(md_welfare_sites_cond(CD, x, p, y, pr$g, pr$a1)$W,
          md_welfare_all(x, p, y, pr$g, pr$a1)) / N
  el <- sapply(s_vec, function(s) md_elast(x, p, y, pr$g, pr$a1, s))
  c(-W, el)                                                         # losses negative, as rmdcev
}
lab <- c(sites, "System closure", paste0("el +", 100 * s_vec, "%"))

## ---- 4. Point estimates ---------------------------------------------------------
pt <- unlist(mdcev_mle$stan_fit$par[c("psi", "gamma", "alpha", "scale")])
names(pt) <- c(paste0("psi[1,", seq_len(J - 1), "]"), paste0("gamma[1,", seq_len(J), "]"),
               "alpha[1,1]", "scale[1]")
res_point <- setNames(md_measures(get_pars(pt), seed = 1), lab)

## ---- 5. The same 100 parameter draws as PrepareSimulationData -------------------
th <- mdcev_mle$stan_fit$theta_tilde
set.seed(898989 + YEAR)
idx <- sample(nrow(th), NSIMS)                       # the draw PrepareSimulationData makes
t0 <- Sys.time()
M <- t(sapply(seq_len(NSIMS), function(r) md_measures(get_pars(th[idx[r], ]), seed = 1000 + r)))
colnames(M) <- lab
cat("time for", NSIMS, "draws:", format(Sys.time() - t0), "\n")

                        ci_lo = apply(M, 2, quantile, 0.025), ci_hi = apply(M, 2, quantile, 0.975),
                        row.names = NULL)

## per-draw results (for the stability tests)

## ---- 6. Sensitivity to the satiation parameters: gamma x 2 and gamma x 4 ------
## Paste at the end of fast_mdcev_YEAR.R (uses th, idx, get_pars, md_measures, lab, YEAR).
## All gammas are scaled by the same factor; the other parameters stay at their draws.
## The conditional errors are recomputed, so each scaled model still fits the observed trips.
## Note: a larger gamma means LESS satiation (trips lose value more slowly).
g_scale <- c(2, 4)
scaled <- lapply(g_scale, function(f) {
  Mf <- t(sapply(seq_len(NSIMS), function(r) {
    pr <- get_pars(th[idx[r], ]); pr$g <- pr$g * f
    md_measures(pr, seed = 1000 + r)
  }))
  colnames(Mf) <- lab
  data.frame(measure = lab, gamma_x = f, mean = colMeans(Mf),
             ci_lo = apply(Mf, 2, quantile, 0.025), ci_hi = apply(Mf, 2, quantile, 0.975),
             row.names = NULL)
})
base <- get(paste0("fast_", YEAR))                     # original gammas (section 5)
gamma_scaling <- rbind(data.frame(measure = base$measure, gamma_x = 1, mean = base$mean,
                                  ci_lo = base$ci_lo, ci_hi = base$ci_hi),
                       do.call(rbind, scaled))
## side by side: original, x2, x4 (means)
print(reshape(gamma_scaling[, c("measure", "gamma_x", "mean")], idvar = "measure",
              timevar = "gamma_x", direction = "wide"), digits = 4, row.names = FALSE)

##########################################################################
## Fast MDCEV welfare and total-trip elasticities, 2021 (gamma profile)
## Same model and the same 100 parameter draws as the rmdcev code, but welfare
## and elasticities are computed directly instead of with mdcev.sim:
##  - conditional errors as in rmdcev (numeraire error zero, visited sites from the
##    first-order conditions, unvisited sites from truncated draws, 100 MLHS draws)
##  - welfare: compensating variation of closing each site one at a time and
##    closing all sites (losses per person, negative as in rmdcev's summary)
##  - total-trip elasticity: all travel costs +1, 10, 20, 50 percent, with the
##    marginal utility of income adjusting so the budget holds
## Requires final_data_2021 in memory.
##########################################################################
suppressMessages({ library(rmdcev); library(dplyr) })
YEAR  <- 2021
s_vec <- c(0.01, 0.10, 0.20, 0.50)

## ---- 1. Estimate (identical to the rmdcev code) ------------------------------
data_model <- mdcev.data(final_data_2021, id.var = "id", alt.var = "parkname", choice = "VisitCount")
mdcev_mle  <- mdcev(~ 1, data = data_model, model = "gamma", algorithm = "MLE",
                    std_errors = "mvn", n_draws = 100, trunc_data = 1, print_iterations = FALSE)

## ---- 2. Functions -------------------------------------------------------------
# conditional demand after travel costs are multiplied by Fm (N x J matrix of factors),
# with income fixed and the marginal utility of income adjusting. For each camper, the new
# numeraire z solves  y - sum_j Fm_j p_j x_j(z) - z = 0, where
# x_j(z) = g_j [ (1 + x_j/g_j) / Fm_j (z / z0)^(1 - a1) - 1 ]  for visited sites (0 if negative).
# The left side falls in z, is positive at z = 0 and non-positive at z = y, so the root is
# bracketed and found by bisection; the budget residual is checked.
md_solve <- function(x, p, y, G, a1, Fm, iters = 200, return_z = FALSE) {
  z0 <- y - rowSums(p * x); stopifnot(all(z0 > 0))
  r <- 1 + x / G; vis <- x > 0
  dem <- function(z) ifelse(vis, pmax(0, G * (r / Fm * (z / z0)^(1 - a1) - 1)), 0)
  excess <- function(z) y - rowSums(Fm * p * dem(z)) - z
  lo <- rep(0, length(y)); hi <- y
  for (it in 1:iters) {
    mid <- (lo + hi) / 2; pos <- excess(mid) > 0
    lo[pos] <- mid[pos]; hi[!pos] <- mid[!pos]
  }
  zs <- (lo + hi) / 2
  if (max(abs(excess(zs))) > 1e-6) stop("md_solve: budget residual too large")
  if (return_z) list(x = dem(zs), z = zs) else dem(zs)
}


# elasticity of total trips when all travel costs rise by s (lambda adjusts)
md_elast <- function(x, p, y, g, a1, s) {
  v <- which(rowSums(x) > 0)
  G <- matrix(g, length(v), ncol(x), byrow = TRUE)
  xn <- md_solve(x[v, , drop = FALSE], p[v, , drop = FALSE], y[v], G, a1,
                 matrix(1 + s, length(v), ncol(x)))
  (sum(xn) / sum(x) - 1) / s
}

# baseline utility (numeraire weight normalised to 1)
md_U0 <- function(x, p, y, G, a1) {
  z <- y - rowSums(p * x)
  psi <- p * (1 + x / G) * z^(a1 - 1)
  list(U0 = rowSums(ifelse(x > 0, psi * G * log1p(x / G), 0)) + z^a1 / a1, psi = psi, z = z)
}

# conditional welfare loss of closing all sites (closed form)
md_welfare_all <- function(x, p, y, g, a1) {
  G <- matrix(g, nrow(x), ncol(x), byrow = TRUE)
  b <- md_U0(x, p, y, G, a1)
  sum((a1 * b$U0)^(1 / a1) - y)
}

# conditional (Hicksian) welfare loss of closing each site one at a time, holding sites not
# visited at baseline at zero trips (this can only raise the loss). For each camper who visited site k: find the numeraire z1 at which utility without
# site k (other visited sites re-optimised) equals baseline utility; the loss is the
# extra spending needed, z1 + sum p_j x_j(z1) - y.
md_welfare_sites <- function(x, p, y, g, a1, iters = 200, detail = FALSE) {
  N <- nrow(x); J <- ncol(x)
  G <- matrix(g, N, J, byrow = TRUE)
  b <- md_U0(x, p, y, G, a1)
  W <- numeric(J); det <- vector("list", J)
  for (k in 1:J) {
    idx <- which(x[, k] > 0)
    if (length(idx) == 0) next
    xs <- x[idx, , drop = FALSE]; ps <- p[idx, , drop = FALSE]; Gs <- G[idx, , drop = FALSE]
    psi <- b$psi[idx, , drop = FALSE]; U0 <- b$U0[idx]
    keep <- (xs > 0); keep[, k] <- FALSE                     # other visited sites
    util <- function(z) {                                     # utility at numeraire z (vector)
      lam <- z^(a1 - 1)
      ratio <- psi / (lam * ps)                               # = 1 + x/G at the optimum
      xj <- ifelse(keep & ratio > 1, Gs * (ratio - 1), 0)
      list(U = rowSums(ifelse(keep & ratio > 1, psi * Gs * log(ratio), 0)) + z^a1 / a1,
           spend = rowSums(ps * xj))
    }
    lo <- b$z[idx]                                            # utility too low here
    hi <- (a1 * U0)^(1 / a1)                                  # numeraire alone reaches U0
    for (it in 1:iters) {
      mid <- (lo + hi) / 2
      u <- util(mid)$U
      lo <- ifelse(u < U0, mid, lo); hi <- ifelse(u < U0, hi, mid)
    }
    z1 <- (lo + hi) / 2
    w_i <- z1 + util(z1)$spend - y[idx]
    W[k] <- sum(w_i)
    if (detail) det[[k]] <- list(idx = idx, z1 = z1, w = w_i)
  }
  if (detail) list(W = W, det = det, U0 = b$U0, R = ifelse(x > 0, b$psi, 0), z0 = b$z) else W
}

# compensating numeraire such that utility at prices P equals U0 (Hicksian), by bisection on
# log t = (1 - a1) log z between lt_lo (utility below U0) and lt_hi (utility above U0).
# Returns z1 and spending on sites at z1.
md_hicks_R <- function(R, P, U0, lt_lo, lt_hi, G, a1, iters = 200) {
  b <- 1 / (1 - a1); RP <- R / P; lRP <- ifelse(RP > 0, log(RP), -Inf)
  util <- function(lt) {
    on <- RP * exp(lt) > 1
    list(U = rowSums(ifelse(on, R * G * (lRP + lt), 0)) + exp(a1 * b * lt) / a1,
         spend = rowSums(ifelse(on, P * G * (RP * exp(lt) - 1), 0)))
  }
  lo <- lt_lo; hi <- lt_hi
  for (it in 1:iters) { mid <- (lo + hi) / 2; low <- util(mid)$U < U0; lo[low] <- mid[low]; hi[!low] <- mid[!low] }
  lt <- (lo + hi) / 2
  list(z1 = exp(b * lt), spend = util(lt)$spend)
}

# Modified Latin hypercube draws (Hess et al., 2006), as in rmdcev: for each of n vectors, D
# uniform draws, one in each interval [(d-1)/D, d/D), in random order. Returns an n x D matrix.
mlhs <- function(n, D) {
  U <- (matrix(rep(0:(D - 1), each = n), n, D) + matrix(runif(n * D), n, D)) / D
  idx <- t(apply(matrix(runif(n * D), n, D), 1, order))
  matrix(U[cbind(rep(1:n, D), as.vector(idx))], n, D)
}

# Conditional error draws, following rmdcev (Lloyd-Smith, 2021): the error of the numeraire is set
# to zero, the errors of visited sites follow from the first-order conditions at the observed trips,
# and the error of each site not visited is drawn from the type I extreme value distribution
# truncated so that the site is not chosen (R_j / p_j <= lambda). This gives
# R_j / (lambda p_j) = (1 + E / w_j)^(-sigma), with E = -log(u), u a modified Latin hypercube draw,
# and w_j = (psi_j / (lambda p_j))^(1/sigma). Sites without trips in the sample are not available.
# (numeraire = "posterior" instead draws the numeraire error from its distribution given the
# observed trips; this was used in an earlier version and is kept for reference.)
# Returns the visited R (N x J) and, for every draw, the unvisited R (N x J x D).
md_cond_draws <- function(x, p, y, g, a1, lpsi, scale, D, numeraire = c("zero", "posterior")) {
  numeraire <- match.arg(numeraire)
  N <- nrow(x); J <- ncol(x); G <- matrix(g, N, J, byrow = TRUE)
  vis <- x > 0; avail <- matrix(colSums(x) > 0, N, J, byrow = TRUE)
  z0 <- y - rowSums(p * x); lam0 <- z0^(a1 - 1)
  R <- ifelse(vis, p * (1 + x / G) * lam0, 0)
  psi <- matrix(exp(lpsi), N, J, byrow = TRUE)
  w <- (psi / (lam0 * p))^(1 / scale)
  unv <- !vis & avail
  K <- rowSums(vis)
  Rd <- array(0, c(N, J, D))
  if (numeraire == "zero") {
    U <- array(mlhs(N * J, D), c(N, J, D))                     # draws for camper i, site j in U[i, j, ]
    for (d in 1:D) Rd[, , d] <- ifelse(unv, lam0 * p * (1 - log(U[, , d]) / w)^(-scale), 0)
  } else {
    A <- 1 + rowSums(ifelse(vis, (psi / R)^(1 / scale), 0)) + rowSums(ifelse(unv, w, 0))
    for (d in 1:D) {
      t <- rgamma(N, shape = K + 1, rate = A)
      E <- matrix(rexp(N * J), N, J)
      Rd[, , d] <- ifelse(unv, lam0 * p * (1 + E / (t * w))^(-scale), 0)
    }
  }
  Rd[K == 0, , ] <- 0                       # campers without trips never start a trip after a cost rise
  list(R = R, Rd = Rd, G = G, z0 = z0, lam0 = lam0,
       Mx = apply(Rd / array(p, c(N, J, D)), c(1, 3), max))       # N x D: highest R_j/p_j among unvisited
}

# conditional welfare loss of closing each site, allowing unvisited sites to enter (same logic:
# the no-entry solution is kept unless an unvisited site would be chosen at its marginal utility
# of income). Returns the losses ($, all campers), the no-entry losses and the share with entry.
md_welfare_sites_cond <- function(CD, x, p, y, g, a1) {
  J <- ncol(x); D <- dim(CD$Rd)[3]
  ne <- md_welfare_sites(x, p, y, g, a1, detail = TRUE)
  W <- ne$W; share <- rep(NA_real_, J)
  for (k in 1:J) {
    d <- ne$det[[k]]; if (is.null(d)) next
    v <- d$idx; wk <- matrix(d$w, length(v), D)
    flag <- CD$Mx[v, , drop = FALSE] > d$z1^(a1 - 1)
    if (any(flag)) {
      fi <- which(flag, arr.ind = TRUE); ii <- v[fi[, 1]]
      Rf <- CD$R[ii, , drop = FALSE] + t(sapply(seq_len(nrow(fi)), function(m) CD$Rd[ii[m], , fi[m, 2]]))
      if (nrow(fi) == 1) Rf <- matrix(Rf, 1)
      Rf[, k] <- 0
      U0 <- ne$U0[ii]
      h <- md_hicks_R(Rf, p[ii, , drop = FALSE], U0, lt_lo = (1 - a1) * log(ne$z0[ii]),
                      lt_hi = (1 - a1) / a1 * log(a1 * U0), CD$G[ii, , drop = FALSE], a1)
      wk[fi] <- h$z1 + h$spend - y[ii]
    }
    W[k] <- sum(wk) / D; share[k] <- mean(flag)
  }
  list(W = W, W_ne = ne$W, share = share)
}

## ---- 3. Data in the order rmdcev uses ----------------------------------------
sd <- mdcev_mle$stan_data
x  <- sd$quant_j; p <- sd$price_j; y <- as.numeric(sd$income)
N  <- sd$I; J <- sd$J
sites <- as.character(data_model$parkname[data_model$id == data_model$id[1]])
stopifnot(length(sites) == J)

## parameters from one row of draws (or the point estimates): site 1 is the base (psi = 0)
get_pars <- function(th) {
  nm <- names(th)
  list(lpsi  = c(0, th[grepl("^psi\\[", nm)]),
       g     = th[grepl("^gamma\\[", nm)],
       a1    = th[grepl("^alpha\\[", nm)][1],
       scale = th[grepl("^scale\\[", nm)][1])
}

## welfare (per person) and total-trip elasticities for one parameter set
md_measures <- function(pr, seed) {
  set.seed(seed)                                                    # MLHS draws for unvisited sites
  CD <- md_cond_draws(x, p, y, pr$g, pr$a1, pr$lpsi, pr$scale, NERRS)
  W  <- c(md_welfare_sites_cond(CD, x, p, y, pr$g, pr$a1)$W,
          md_welfare_all(x, p, y, pr$g, pr$a1)) / N
  el <- sapply(s_vec, function(s) md_elast(x, p, y, pr$g, pr$a1, s))
  c(-W, el)                                                         # losses negative, as rmdcev
}
lab <- c(sites, "System closure", paste0("el +", 100 * s_vec, "%"))

## ---- 4. Point estimates ---------------------------------------------------------
pt <- unlist(mdcev_mle$stan_fit$par[c("psi", "gamma", "alpha", "scale")])
names(pt) <- c(paste0("psi[1,", seq_len(J - 1), "]"), paste0("gamma[1,", seq_len(J), "]"),
               "alpha[1,1]", "scale[1]")
res_point <- setNames(md_measures(get_pars(pt), seed = 1), lab)

## ---- 5. The same 100 parameter draws as PrepareSimulationData -------------------
th <- mdcev_mle$stan_fit$theta_tilde
set.seed(898989 + YEAR)
idx <- sample(nrow(th), NSIMS)                       # the draw PrepareSimulationData makes
t0 <- Sys.time()

colnames(M) <- lab
cat("time for", NSIMS, "draws:", format(Sys.time() - t0), "\n")

                        row.names = NULL)

## ---- 6. Sensitivity to the satiation parameters: gamma x 2 and gamma x 4 ------
## Paste at the end of fast_mdcev_YEAR.R (uses th, idx, get_pars, md_measures, lab, YEAR).
## All gammas are scaled by the same factor; the other parameters stay at their draws.
## The conditional errors are recomputed, so each scaled model still fits the observed trips.
## Note: a larger gamma means LESS satiation (trips lose value more slowly).
g_scale <- c(2, 4)
scaled <- lapply(g_scale, function(f) {
  Mf <- t(sapply(seq_len(NSIMS), function(r) {
    pr <- get_pars(th[idx[r], ]); pr$g <- pr$g * f
    md_measures(pr, seed = 1000 + r)
  }))
  colnames(Mf) <- lab
  data.frame(measure = lab, gamma_x = f, mean = colMeans(Mf),
             ci_lo = apply(Mf, 2, quantile, 0.025), ci_hi = apply(Mf, 2, quantile, 0.975),
             row.names = NULL)
})
base <- get(paste0("fast_", YEAR))                     # original gammas (section 5)

                       do.call(rbind, scaled))

##########################################################################
## Fast MDCEV welfare and total-trip elasticities, 2022 (gamma profile)
## Same model and the same 100 parameter draws as the rmdcev code, but welfare
## and elasticities are computed directly instead of with mdcev.sim:
##  - conditional errors as in rmdcev (numeraire error zero, visited sites from the
##    first-order conditions, unvisited sites from truncated draws, 100 MLHS draws)
##  - welfare: compensating variation of closing each site one at a time and
##    closing all sites (losses per person, negative as in rmdcev's summary)
##  - total-trip elasticity: all travel costs +1, 10, 20, 50 percent, with the
##    marginal utility of income adjusting so the budget holds
## Requires final_data_2019 in memory.
##########################################################################
suppressMessages({ library(rmdcev); library(dplyr) })
YEAR  <- 2022
s_vec <- c(0.01, 0.10, 0.20, 0.50)

## ---- 1. Estimate (identical to the rmdcev code) ------------------------------
data_model <- mdcev.data(final_data_2022, id.var = "id", alt.var = "parkname", choice = "VisitCount")
mdcev_mle  <- mdcev(~ 1, data = data_model, model = "gamma", algorithm = "MLE",
                    std_errors = "mvn", n_draws = 100, trunc_data = 1, print_iterations = FALSE)

## ---- 2. Functions -------------------------------------------------------------
# conditional demand after travel costs are multiplied by Fm (N x J matrix of factors),
# with income fixed and the marginal utility of income adjusting. For each camper, the new
# numeraire z solves  y - sum_j Fm_j p_j x_j(z) - z = 0, where
# x_j(z) = g_j [ (1 + x_j/g_j) / Fm_j (z / z0)^(1 - a1) - 1 ]  for visited sites (0 if negative).
# The left side falls in z, is positive at z = 0 and non-positive at z = y, so the root is
# bracketed and found by bisection; the budget residual is checked.
md_solve <- function(x, p, y, G, a1, Fm, iters = 200, return_z = FALSE) {
  z0 <- y - rowSums(p * x); stopifnot(all(z0 > 0))
  r <- 1 + x / G; vis <- x > 0
  dem <- function(z) ifelse(vis, pmax(0, G * (r / Fm * (z / z0)^(1 - a1) - 1)), 0)
  excess <- function(z) y - rowSums(Fm * p * dem(z)) - z
  lo <- rep(0, length(y)); hi <- y
  for (it in 1:iters) {
    mid <- (lo + hi) / 2; pos <- excess(mid) > 0
    lo[pos] <- mid[pos]; hi[!pos] <- mid[!pos]
  }
  zs <- (lo + hi) / 2
  if (max(abs(excess(zs))) > 1e-6) stop("md_solve: budget residual too large")
  if (return_z) list(x = dem(zs), z = zs) else dem(zs)
}


# elasticity of total trips when all travel costs rise by s (lambda adjusts)
md_elast <- function(x, p, y, g, a1, s) {
  v <- which(rowSums(x) > 0)
  G <- matrix(g, length(v), ncol(x), byrow = TRUE)
  xn <- md_solve(x[v, , drop = FALSE], p[v, , drop = FALSE], y[v], G, a1,
                 matrix(1 + s, length(v), ncol(x)))
  (sum(xn) / sum(x) - 1) / s
}

# baseline utility (numeraire weight normalised to 1)
md_U0 <- function(x, p, y, G, a1) {
  z <- y - rowSums(p * x)
  psi <- p * (1 + x / G) * z^(a1 - 1)
  list(U0 = rowSums(ifelse(x > 0, psi * G * log1p(x / G), 0)) + z^a1 / a1, psi = psi, z = z)
}

# conditional welfare loss of closing all sites (closed form)
md_welfare_all <- function(x, p, y, g, a1) {
  G <- matrix(g, nrow(x), ncol(x), byrow = TRUE)
  b <- md_U0(x, p, y, G, a1)
  sum((a1 * b$U0)^(1 / a1) - y)
}

# conditional (Hicksian) welfare loss of closing each site one at a time, holding sites not
# visited at baseline at zero trips (this can only raise the loss). For each camper who visited site k: find the numeraire z1 at which utility without
# site k (other visited sites re-optimised) equals baseline utility; the loss is the
# extra spending needed, z1 + sum p_j x_j(z1) - y.
md_welfare_sites <- function(x, p, y, g, a1, iters = 200, detail = FALSE) {
  N <- nrow(x); J <- ncol(x)
  G <- matrix(g, N, J, byrow = TRUE)
  b <- md_U0(x, p, y, G, a1)
  W <- numeric(J); det <- vector("list", J)
  for (k in 1:J) {
    idx <- which(x[, k] > 0)
    if (length(idx) == 0) next
    xs <- x[idx, , drop = FALSE]; ps <- p[idx, , drop = FALSE]; Gs <- G[idx, , drop = FALSE]
    psi <- b$psi[idx, , drop = FALSE]; U0 <- b$U0[idx]
    keep <- (xs > 0); keep[, k] <- FALSE                     # other visited sites
    util <- function(z) {                                     # utility at numeraire z (vector)
      lam <- z^(a1 - 1)
      ratio <- psi / (lam * ps)                               # = 1 + x/G at the optimum
      xj <- ifelse(keep & ratio > 1, Gs * (ratio - 1), 0)
      list(U = rowSums(ifelse(keep & ratio > 1, psi * Gs * log(ratio), 0)) + z^a1 / a1,
           spend = rowSums(ps * xj))
    }
    lo <- b$z[idx]                                            # utility too low here
    hi <- (a1 * U0)^(1 / a1)                                  # numeraire alone reaches U0
    for (it in 1:iters) {
      mid <- (lo + hi) / 2
      u <- util(mid)$U
      lo <- ifelse(u < U0, mid, lo); hi <- ifelse(u < U0, hi, mid)
    }
    z1 <- (lo + hi) / 2
    w_i <- z1 + util(z1)$spend - y[idx]
    W[k] <- sum(w_i)
    if (detail) det[[k]] <- list(idx = idx, z1 = z1, w = w_i)
  }
  if (detail) list(W = W, det = det, U0 = b$U0, R = ifelse(x > 0, b$psi, 0), z0 = b$z) else W
}

# compensating numeraire such that utility at prices P equals U0 (Hicksian), by bisection on
# log t = (1 - a1) log z between lt_lo (utility below U0) and lt_hi (utility above U0).
# Returns z1 and spending on sites at z1.
md_hicks_R <- function(R, P, U0, lt_lo, lt_hi, G, a1, iters = 200) {
  b <- 1 / (1 - a1); RP <- R / P; lRP <- ifelse(RP > 0, log(RP), -Inf)
  util <- function(lt) {
    on <- RP * exp(lt) > 1
    list(U = rowSums(ifelse(on, R * G * (lRP + lt), 0)) + exp(a1 * b * lt) / a1,
         spend = rowSums(ifelse(on, P * G * (RP * exp(lt) - 1), 0)))
  }
  lo <- lt_lo; hi <- lt_hi
  for (it in 1:iters) { mid <- (lo + hi) / 2; low <- util(mid)$U < U0; lo[low] <- mid[low]; hi[!low] <- mid[!low] }
  lt <- (lo + hi) / 2
  list(z1 = exp(b * lt), spend = util(lt)$spend)
}

# Modified Latin hypercube draws (Hess et al., 2006), as in rmdcev: for each of n vectors, D
# uniform draws, one in each interval [(d-1)/D, d/D), in random order. Returns an n x D matrix.
mlhs <- function(n, D) {
  U <- (matrix(rep(0:(D - 1), each = n), n, D) + matrix(runif(n * D), n, D)) / D
  idx <- t(apply(matrix(runif(n * D), n, D), 1, order))
  matrix(U[cbind(rep(1:n, D), as.vector(idx))], n, D)
}

# Conditional error draws, following rmdcev (Lloyd-Smith, 2021): the error of the numeraire is set
# to zero, the errors of visited sites follow from the first-order conditions at the observed trips,
# and the error of each site not visited is drawn from the type I extreme value distribution
# truncated so that the site is not chosen (R_j / p_j <= lambda). This gives
# R_j / (lambda p_j) = (1 + E / w_j)^(-sigma), with E = -log(u), u a modified Latin hypercube draw,
# and w_j = (psi_j / (lambda p_j))^(1/sigma). Sites without trips in the sample are not available.
# (numeraire = "posterior" instead draws the numeraire error from its distribution given the
# observed trips; this was used in an earlier version and is kept for reference.)
# Returns the visited R (N x J) and, for every draw, the unvisited R (N x J x D).
md_cond_draws <- function(x, p, y, g, a1, lpsi, scale, D, numeraire = c("zero", "posterior")) {
  numeraire <- match.arg(numeraire)
  N <- nrow(x); J <- ncol(x); G <- matrix(g, N, J, byrow = TRUE)
  vis <- x > 0; avail <- matrix(colSums(x) > 0, N, J, byrow = TRUE)
  z0 <- y - rowSums(p * x); lam0 <- z0^(a1 - 1)
  R <- ifelse(vis, p * (1 + x / G) * lam0, 0)
  psi <- matrix(exp(lpsi), N, J, byrow = TRUE)
  w <- (psi / (lam0 * p))^(1 / scale)
  unv <- !vis & avail
  K <- rowSums(vis)
  Rd <- array(0, c(N, J, D))
  if (numeraire == "zero") {
    U <- array(mlhs(N * J, D), c(N, J, D))                     # draws for camper i, site j in U[i, j, ]
    for (d in 1:D) Rd[, , d] <- ifelse(unv, lam0 * p * (1 - log(U[, , d]) / w)^(-scale), 0)
  } else {
    A <- 1 + rowSums(ifelse(vis, (psi / R)^(1 / scale), 0)) + rowSums(ifelse(unv, w, 0))
    for (d in 1:D) {
      t <- rgamma(N, shape = K + 1, rate = A)
      E <- matrix(rexp(N * J), N, J)
      Rd[, , d] <- ifelse(unv, lam0 * p * (1 + E / (t * w))^(-scale), 0)
    }
  }
  Rd[K == 0, , ] <- 0                       # campers without trips never start a trip after a cost rise
  list(R = R, Rd = Rd, G = G, z0 = z0, lam0 = lam0,
       Mx = apply(Rd / array(p, c(N, J, D)), c(1, 3), max))       # N x D: highest R_j/p_j among unvisited
}

# conditional welfare loss of closing each site, allowing unvisited sites to enter (same logic:
# the no-entry solution is kept unless an unvisited site would be chosen at its marginal utility
# of income). Returns the losses ($, all campers), the no-entry losses and the share with entry.
md_welfare_sites_cond <- function(CD, x, p, y, g, a1) {
  J <- ncol(x); D <- dim(CD$Rd)[3]
  ne <- md_welfare_sites(x, p, y, g, a1, detail = TRUE)
  W <- ne$W; share <- rep(NA_real_, J)
  for (k in 1:J) {
    d <- ne$det[[k]]; if (is.null(d)) next
    v <- d$idx; wk <- matrix(d$w, length(v), D)
    flag <- CD$Mx[v, , drop = FALSE] > d$z1^(a1 - 1)
    if (any(flag)) {
      fi <- which(flag, arr.ind = TRUE); ii <- v[fi[, 1]]
      Rf <- CD$R[ii, , drop = FALSE] + t(sapply(seq_len(nrow(fi)), function(m) CD$Rd[ii[m], , fi[m, 2]]))
      if (nrow(fi) == 1) Rf <- matrix(Rf, 1)
      Rf[, k] <- 0
      U0 <- ne$U0[ii]
      h <- md_hicks_R(Rf, p[ii, , drop = FALSE], U0, lt_lo = (1 - a1) * log(ne$z0[ii]),
                      lt_hi = (1 - a1) / a1 * log(a1 * U0), CD$G[ii, , drop = FALSE], a1)
      wk[fi] <- h$z1 + h$spend - y[ii]
    }
    W[k] <- sum(wk) / D; share[k] <- mean(flag)
  }
  list(W = W, W_ne = ne$W, share = share)
}

## ---- 3. Data in the order rmdcev uses ----------------------------------------
sd <- mdcev_mle$stan_data
x  <- sd$quant_j; p <- sd$price_j; y <- as.numeric(sd$income)
N  <- sd$I; J <- sd$J
sites <- as.character(data_model$parkname[data_model$id == data_model$id[1]])
stopifnot(length(sites) == J)

## parameters from one row of draws (or the point estimates): site 1 is the base (psi = 0)
get_pars <- function(th) {
  nm <- names(th)
  list(lpsi  = c(0, th[grepl("^psi\\[", nm)]),
       g     = th[grepl("^gamma\\[", nm)],
       a1    = th[grepl("^alpha\\[", nm)][1],
       scale = th[grepl("^scale\\[", nm)][1])
}

## welfare (per person) and total-trip elasticities for one parameter set
md_measures <- function(pr, seed) {
  set.seed(seed)                                                    # MLHS draws for unvisited sites
  CD <- md_cond_draws(x, p, y, pr$g, pr$a1, pr$lpsi, pr$scale, NERRS)
  W  <- c(md_welfare_sites_cond(CD, x, p, y, pr$g, pr$a1)$W,
          md_welfare_all(x, p, y, pr$g, pr$a1)) / N
  el <- sapply(s_vec, function(s) md_elast(x, p, y, pr$g, pr$a1, s))
  c(-W, el)                                                         # losses negative, as rmdcev
}
lab <- c(sites, "System closure", paste0("el +", 100 * s_vec, "%"))

## ---- 4. Point estimates ---------------------------------------------------------
pt <- unlist(mdcev_mle$stan_fit$par[c("psi", "gamma", "alpha", "scale")])
names(pt) <- c(paste0("psi[1,", seq_len(J - 1), "]"), paste0("gamma[1,", seq_len(J), "]"),
               "alpha[1,1]", "scale[1]")
res_point <- setNames(md_measures(get_pars(pt), seed = 1), lab)

## ---- 6. Sensitivity to the satiation parameters: gamma x 2 and gamma x 4 ------
## Paste at the end of fast_mdcev_YEAR.R (uses th, idx, get_pars, md_measures, lab, YEAR).
## All gammas are scaled by the same factor; the other parameters stay at their draws.
## The conditional errors are recomputed, so each scaled model still fits the observed trips.
## Note: a larger gamma means LESS satiation (trips lose value more slowly).
g_scale <- c(2, 4)
scaled <- lapply(g_scale, function(f) {
  Mf <- t(sapply(seq_len(NSIMS), function(r) {
    pr <- get_pars(th[idx[r], ]); pr$g <- pr$g * f
    md_measures(pr, seed = 1000 + r)
  }))
  colnames(Mf) <- lab
  data.frame(measure = lab, gamma_x = f, mean = colMeans(Mf),
             ci_lo = apply(Mf, 2, quantile, 0.025), ci_hi = apply(Mf, 2, quantile, 0.975),
             row.names = NULL)
})
base <- get(paste0("fast_", YEAR))                     # original gammas (section 5)
gamma_scaling <- rbind(data.frame(measure = base$measure, gamma_x = 1, mean = base$mean,
                                  ci_lo = base$ci_lo, ci_hi = base$ci_hi),
                       do.call(rbind, scaled))
## side by side: original, x2, x4 (means)

# 2023 - MDCEV #

##########################################################################
## Fast MDCEV welfare and total-trip elasticities, 2023 (gamma profile)
## Same model and the same 100 parameter draws as the rmdcev code, but welfare
## and elasticities are computed directly instead of with mdcev.sim:
##  - conditional errors as in rmdcev (numeraire error zero, visited sites from the
##    first-order conditions, unvisited sites from truncated draws, 100 MLHS draws)
##  - welfare: compensating variation of closing each site one at a time and
##    closing all sites (losses per person, negative as in rmdcev's summary)
##  - total-trip elasticity: all travel costs +1, 10, 20, 50 percent, with the
##    marginal utility of income adjusting so the budget holds
## Requires final_data_2023 in memory.
##########################################################################
suppressMessages({ library(rmdcev); library(dplyr) })
YEAR  <- 2023
s_vec <- c(0.01, 0.10, 0.20, 0.50)

## ---- 1. Estimate (identical to the rmdcev code) ------------------------------
data_model <- mdcev.data(final_data_2023, id.var = "id", alt.var = "parkname", choice = "VisitCount")
mdcev_mle  <- mdcev(~ 1, data = data_model, model = "gamma", algorithm = "MLE",
                    std_errors = "mvn", n_draws = 100, trunc_data = 1, print_iterations = FALSE)

## ---- 2. Functions -------------------------------------------------------------
# conditional demand after travel costs are multiplied by Fm (N x J matrix of factors),
# with income fixed and the marginal utility of income adjusting. For each camper, the new
# numeraire z solves  y - sum_j Fm_j p_j x_j(z) - z = 0, where
# x_j(z) = g_j [ (1 + x_j/g_j) / Fm_j (z / z0)^(1 - a1) - 1 ]  for visited sites (0 if negative).
# The left side falls in z, is positive at z = 0 and non-positive at z = y, so the root is
# bracketed and found by bisection; the budget residual is checked.
md_solve <- function(x, p, y, G, a1, Fm, iters = 200, return_z = FALSE) {
  z0 <- y - rowSums(p * x); stopifnot(all(z0 > 0))
  r <- 1 + x / G; vis <- x > 0
  dem <- function(z) ifelse(vis, pmax(0, G * (r / Fm * (z / z0)^(1 - a1) - 1)), 0)
  excess <- function(z) y - rowSums(Fm * p * dem(z)) - z
  lo <- rep(0, length(y)); hi <- y
  for (it in 1:iters) {
    mid <- (lo + hi) / 2; pos <- excess(mid) > 0
    lo[pos] <- mid[pos]; hi[!pos] <- mid[!pos]
  }
  zs <- (lo + hi) / 2
  if (max(abs(excess(zs))) > 1e-6) stop("md_solve: budget residual too large")
  if (return_z) list(x = dem(zs), z = zs) else dem(zs)
}


# elasticity of total trips when all travel costs rise by s (lambda adjusts)
md_elast <- function(x, p, y, g, a1, s) {
  v <- which(rowSums(x) > 0)
  G <- matrix(g, length(v), ncol(x), byrow = TRUE)
  xn <- md_solve(x[v, , drop = FALSE], p[v, , drop = FALSE], y[v], G, a1,
                 matrix(1 + s, length(v), ncol(x)))
  (sum(xn) / sum(x) - 1) / s
}

# baseline utility (numeraire weight normalised to 1)
md_U0 <- function(x, p, y, G, a1) {
  z <- y - rowSums(p * x)
  psi <- p * (1 + x / G) * z^(a1 - 1)
  list(U0 = rowSums(ifelse(x > 0, psi * G * log1p(x / G), 0)) + z^a1 / a1, psi = psi, z = z)
}

# conditional welfare loss of closing all sites (closed form)
md_welfare_all <- function(x, p, y, g, a1) {
  G <- matrix(g, nrow(x), ncol(x), byrow = TRUE)
  b <- md_U0(x, p, y, G, a1)
  sum((a1 * b$U0)^(1 / a1) - y)
}

# conditional (Hicksian) welfare loss of closing each site one at a time, holding sites not
# visited at baseline at zero trips (this can only raise the loss). For each camper who visited site k: find the numeraire z1 at which utility without
# site k (other visited sites re-optimised) equals baseline utility; the loss is the
# extra spending needed, z1 + sum p_j x_j(z1) - y.
md_welfare_sites <- function(x, p, y, g, a1, iters = 200, detail = FALSE) {
  N <- nrow(x); J <- ncol(x)
  G <- matrix(g, N, J, byrow = TRUE)
  b <- md_U0(x, p, y, G, a1)
  W <- numeric(J); det <- vector("list", J)
  for (k in 1:J) {
    idx <- which(x[, k] > 0)
    if (length(idx) == 0) next
    xs <- x[idx, , drop = FALSE]; ps <- p[idx, , drop = FALSE]; Gs <- G[idx, , drop = FALSE]
    psi <- b$psi[idx, , drop = FALSE]; U0 <- b$U0[idx]
    keep <- (xs > 0); keep[, k] <- FALSE                     # other visited sites
    util <- function(z) {                                     # utility at numeraire z (vector)
      lam <- z^(a1 - 1)
      ratio <- psi / (lam * ps)                               # = 1 + x/G at the optimum
      xj <- ifelse(keep & ratio > 1, Gs * (ratio - 1), 0)
      list(U = rowSums(ifelse(keep & ratio > 1, psi * Gs * log(ratio), 0)) + z^a1 / a1,
           spend = rowSums(ps * xj))
    }
    lo <- b$z[idx]                                            # utility too low here
    hi <- (a1 * U0)^(1 / a1)                                  # numeraire alone reaches U0
    for (it in 1:iters) {
      mid <- (lo + hi) / 2
      u <- util(mid)$U
      lo <- ifelse(u < U0, mid, lo); hi <- ifelse(u < U0, hi, mid)
    }
    z1 <- (lo + hi) / 2
    w_i <- z1 + util(z1)$spend - y[idx]
    W[k] <- sum(w_i)
    if (detail) det[[k]] <- list(idx = idx, z1 = z1, w = w_i)
  }
  if (detail) list(W = W, det = det, U0 = b$U0, R = ifelse(x > 0, b$psi, 0), z0 = b$z) else W
}

# compensating numeraire such that utility at prices P equals U0 (Hicksian), by bisection on
# log t = (1 - a1) log z between lt_lo (utility below U0) and lt_hi (utility above U0).
# Returns z1 and spending on sites at z1.
md_hicks_R <- function(R, P, U0, lt_lo, lt_hi, G, a1, iters = 200) {
  b <- 1 / (1 - a1); RP <- R / P; lRP <- ifelse(RP > 0, log(RP), -Inf)
  util <- function(lt) {
    on <- RP * exp(lt) > 1
    list(U = rowSums(ifelse(on, R * G * (lRP + lt), 0)) + exp(a1 * b * lt) / a1,
         spend = rowSums(ifelse(on, P * G * (RP * exp(lt) - 1), 0)))
  }
  lo <- lt_lo; hi <- lt_hi
  for (it in 1:iters) { mid <- (lo + hi) / 2; low <- util(mid)$U < U0; lo[low] <- mid[low]; hi[!low] <- mid[!low] }
  lt <- (lo + hi) / 2
  list(z1 = exp(b * lt), spend = util(lt)$spend)
}

# Modified Latin hypercube draws (Hess et al., 2006), as in rmdcev: for each of n vectors, D
# uniform draws, one in each interval [(d-1)/D, d/D), in random order. Returns an n x D matrix.
mlhs <- function(n, D) {
  U <- (matrix(rep(0:(D - 1), each = n), n, D) + matrix(runif(n * D), n, D)) / D
  idx <- t(apply(matrix(runif(n * D), n, D), 1, order))
  matrix(U[cbind(rep(1:n, D), as.vector(idx))], n, D)
}

# Conditional error draws, following rmdcev (Lloyd-Smith, 2021): the error of the numeraire is set
# to zero, the errors of visited sites follow from the first-order conditions at the observed trips,
# and the error of each site not visited is drawn from the type I extreme value distribution
# truncated so that the site is not chosen (R_j / p_j <= lambda). This gives
# R_j / (lambda p_j) = (1 + E / w_j)^(-sigma), with E = -log(u), u a modified Latin hypercube draw,
# and w_j = (psi_j / (lambda p_j))^(1/sigma). Sites without trips in the sample are not available.
# (numeraire = "posterior" instead draws the numeraire error from its distribution given the
# observed trips; this was used in an earlier version and is kept for reference.)
# Returns the visited R (N x J) and, for every draw, the unvisited R (N x J x D).
md_cond_draws <- function(x, p, y, g, a1, lpsi, scale, D, numeraire = c("zero", "posterior")) {
  numeraire <- match.arg(numeraire)
  N <- nrow(x); J <- ncol(x); G <- matrix(g, N, J, byrow = TRUE)
  vis <- x > 0; avail <- matrix(colSums(x) > 0, N, J, byrow = TRUE)
  z0 <- y - rowSums(p * x); lam0 <- z0^(a1 - 1)
  R <- ifelse(vis, p * (1 + x / G) * lam0, 0)
  psi <- matrix(exp(lpsi), N, J, byrow = TRUE)
  w <- (psi / (lam0 * p))^(1 / scale)
  unv <- !vis & avail
  K <- rowSums(vis)
  Rd <- array(0, c(N, J, D))
  if (numeraire == "zero") {
    U <- array(mlhs(N * J, D), c(N, J, D))                     # draws for camper i, site j in U[i, j, ]
    for (d in 1:D) Rd[, , d] <- ifelse(unv, lam0 * p * (1 - log(U[, , d]) / w)^(-scale), 0)
  } else {
    A <- 1 + rowSums(ifelse(vis, (psi / R)^(1 / scale), 0)) + rowSums(ifelse(unv, w, 0))
    for (d in 1:D) {
      t <- rgamma(N, shape = K + 1, rate = A)
      E <- matrix(rexp(N * J), N, J)
      Rd[, , d] <- ifelse(unv, lam0 * p * (1 + E / (t * w))^(-scale), 0)
    }
  }
  Rd[K == 0, , ] <- 0                       # campers without trips never start a trip after a cost rise
  list(R = R, Rd = Rd, G = G, z0 = z0, lam0 = lam0,
       Mx = apply(Rd / array(p, c(N, J, D)), c(1, 3), max))       # N x D: highest R_j/p_j among unvisited
}

# conditional welfare loss of closing each site, allowing unvisited sites to enter (same logic:
# the no-entry solution is kept unless an unvisited site would be chosen at its marginal utility
# of income). Returns the losses ($, all campers), the no-entry losses and the share with entry.
md_welfare_sites_cond <- function(CD, x, p, y, g, a1) {
  J <- ncol(x); D <- dim(CD$Rd)[3]
  ne <- md_welfare_sites(x, p, y, g, a1, detail = TRUE)
  W <- ne$W; share <- rep(NA_real_, J)
  for (k in 1:J) {
    d <- ne$det[[k]]; if (is.null(d)) next
    v <- d$idx; wk <- matrix(d$w, length(v), D)
    flag <- CD$Mx[v, , drop = FALSE] > d$z1^(a1 - 1)
    if (any(flag)) {
      fi <- which(flag, arr.ind = TRUE); ii <- v[fi[, 1]]
      Rf <- CD$R[ii, , drop = FALSE] + t(sapply(seq_len(nrow(fi)), function(m) CD$Rd[ii[m], , fi[m, 2]]))
      if (nrow(fi) == 1) Rf <- matrix(Rf, 1)
      Rf[, k] <- 0
      U0 <- ne$U0[ii]
      h <- md_hicks_R(Rf, p[ii, , drop = FALSE], U0, lt_lo = (1 - a1) * log(ne$z0[ii]),
                      lt_hi = (1 - a1) / a1 * log(a1 * U0), CD$G[ii, , drop = FALSE], a1)
      wk[fi] <- h$z1 + h$spend - y[ii]
    }
    W[k] <- sum(wk) / D; share[k] <- mean(flag)
  }
  list(W = W, W_ne = ne$W, share = share)
}

## ---- 3. Data in the order rmdcev uses ----------------------------------------
sd <- mdcev_mle$stan_data
x  <- sd$quant_j; p <- sd$price_j; y <- as.numeric(sd$income)
N  <- sd$I; J <- sd$J
sites <- as.character(data_model$parkname[data_model$id == data_model$id[1]])
stopifnot(length(sites) == J)

## parameters from one row of draws (or the point estimates): site 1 is the base (psi = 0)
get_pars <- function(th) {
  nm <- names(th)
  list(lpsi  = c(0, th[grepl("^psi\\[", nm)]),
       g     = th[grepl("^gamma\\[", nm)],
       a1    = th[grepl("^alpha\\[", nm)][1],
       scale = th[grepl("^scale\\[", nm)][1])
}

## welfare (per person) and total-trip elasticities for one parameter set
md_measures <- function(pr, seed) {
  set.seed(seed)                                                    # MLHS draws for unvisited sites
  CD <- md_cond_draws(x, p, y, pr$g, pr$a1, pr$lpsi, pr$scale, NERRS)
  W  <- c(md_welfare_sites_cond(CD, x, p, y, pr$g, pr$a1)$W,
          md_welfare_all(x, p, y, pr$g, pr$a1)) / N
  el <- sapply(s_vec, function(s) md_elast(x, p, y, pr$g, pr$a1, s))
  c(-W, el)                                                         # losses negative, as rmdcev
}
lab <- c(sites, "System closure", paste0("el +", 100 * s_vec, "%"))

names(pt) <- c(paste0("psi[1,", seq_len(J - 1), "]"), paste0("gamma[1,", seq_len(J), "]"),
               "alpha[1,1]", "scale[1]")
res_point <- setNames(md_measures(get_pars(pt), seed = 1), lab)

## ---- 6. Sensitivity to the satiation parameters: gamma x 2 and gamma x 4 ------
## Paste at the end of fast_mdcev_YEAR.R (uses th, idx, get_pars, md_measures, lab, YEAR).
## All gammas are scaled by the same factor; the other parameters stay at their draws.
## The conditional errors are recomputed, so each scaled model still fits the observed trips.
## Note: a larger gamma means LESS satiation (trips lose value more slowly).
g_scale <- c(2, 4)
scaled <- lapply(g_scale, function(f) {
  Mf <- t(sapply(seq_len(NSIMS), function(r) {
    pr <- get_pars(th[idx[r], ]); pr$g <- pr$g * f
    md_measures(pr, seed = 1000 + r)
  }))
  colnames(Mf) <- lab
  data.frame(measure = lab, gamma_x = f, mean = colMeans(Mf),
             ci_lo = apply(Mf, 2, quantile, 0.025), ci_hi = apply(Mf, 2, quantile, 0.975),
             row.names = NULL)
})
base <- get(paste0("fast_", YEAR))                     # original gammas (section 5)
gamma_scaling <- rbind(data.frame(measure = base$measure, gamma_x = 1, mean = base$mean,
                                  ci_lo = base$ci_lo, ci_hi = base$ci_hi),
                       do.call(rbind, scaled))
## side by side: original, x2, x4 (means)
