All Comparison

So pull we pull parameters from the original code and run the welfare from the manual that runs faster.

suppressMessages(library(rmdcev))
 
compare_same_draws <- function(YEAR, nerrs = 100) {
  yr  <- as.character(YEAR)
  mod <- mdcev_model_list[[yr]]
  if (is.null(mod)) stop("mdcev_model_list[[\"", yr, "\"]] not found")
  wobj <- get(paste0("welfare_", yr))
 
  ## ---- data in the order rmdcev uses, for this year ----------------------------------
  sd <- mod$stan_data
  x <- sd$quant_j; p <- sd$price_j; y <- as.numeric(sd$income); N <- sd$I; J <- sd$J
  dm <- mdcev.data(get(paste0("final_data_", yr)), id.var = "id", alt.var = "parkname",
                   choice = "VisitCount")
  sites <- as.character(dm$parkname[dm$id == dm$id[1]])
  cat("\n=====", yr, ": N =", N, " J =", J, " length(welfare) =", length(wobj), "=====\n")
  stopifnot(length(sites) == J, is.list(wobj), length(wobj) == N)
 
  ## ---- rmdcev summary in numeric policy order ------------------------------------------
  rm_tab  <- as.data.frame(summary(wobj)$CoefTable)
  pol_col <- names(rm_tab)[!sapply(rm_tab, is.numeric)][1]
  pol_num <- as.integer(gsub("\\D", "", rm_tab[[pol_col]]))
  stopifnot(identical(sort(pol_num), 1:(J + 1)))    # fails if the policies were not J + 1
  rm_tab  <- rm_tab[order(pol_num), ]
  num  <- rm_tab[sapply(rm_tab, is.numeric)]
  pick <- function(pattern) num[[grep(pattern, names(num), ignore.case = TRUE)[1]]]
  rm_mean <- pick("mean"); rm_lo <- pick("lo"); rm_hi <- pick("hi")
 
  ## ---- rmdcev welfare for each parameter draw ------------------------------------------
  rm_draws <- Reduce(`+`, lapply(wobj, function(m) as.matrix(m)[, 1:(J + 1)])) / N
 
  ## ---- which rows of theta_tilde rmdcev used (matched on the closed-form system closure)
  th_rm   <- mod$stan_fit$theta_tilde
  sys_all <- apply(th_rm, 1, function(r) {
    pr <- get_pars(r); -md_welfare_all(x, p, y, pr$g, pr$a1) / N
  })
  rm_sys <- rm_draws[, J + 1]
  gap    <- sapply(rm_sys, function(v) min(abs(v - sys_all)))
  rm_idx <- sapply(rm_sys, function(v) which.min(abs(v - sys_all)))
  cat("rmdcev draws reproduced by a theta_tilde row:", sum(gap < 1e-4), "of", length(rm_sys), "\n")
  stopifnot(all(gap < 1e-4))
 
  ## ---- manual welfare on exactly those draws ------------------------------------------
  welf_one <- function(pr, seed) {
    set.seed(seed)
    CD  <- md_cond_draws(x, p, y, pr$g, pr$a1, pr$lpsi, pr$scale, nerrs)
    out <- md_welfare_sites_cond(CD, x, p, y, pr$g, pr$a1)
    list(W = -c(out$W, md_welfare_all(x, p, y, pr$g, pr$a1)) / N, share = out$share)
  }
  t0 <- Sys.time()
  rm_res <- lapply(seq_along(rm_idx), function(r)
              welf_one(get_pars(th_rm[rm_idx[r], ]), seed = 1000 + r))
  cat("time for", length(rm_idx), "draws:", format(Sys.time() - t0), "\n")
 
  man_draws <- t(sapply(rm_res, `[[`, "W"))
  entry     <- t(sapply(rm_res, `[[`, "share"))
  colnames(man_draws) <- colnames(rm_draws) <- c(sites, "System closure")
  colnames(entry) <- sites
 
  ## ---- compare ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  man_mean <- colMeans(man_draws)
  cmp <- data.frame(
    year          = YEAR,
    measure       = c(sites, "System closure"),
    rmdcev        = rm_mean,
    manual        = man_mean,
    pct_diff      = 100 * (man_mean - rm_mean) / abs(rm_mean),
    max_draw_diff = apply(abs(man_draws - rm_draws), 2, max),
    rm_ci         = sprintf("[%.2f, %.2f]", rm_lo, rm_hi),
    man_ci        = sprintf("[%.2f, %.2f]", apply(man_draws, 2, quantile, 0.025),
                                            apply(man_draws, 2, quantile, 0.975))
  )
  print(cmp[, -1], digits = 4, row.names = FALSE)
 
  s <- 1:J
  overview <- data.frame(
    year              = YEAR,
    N                 = N,
    J                 = J,
    draws             = length(rm_idx),
    mean_park_rmdcev  = mean(rm_mean[s]),
    mean_park_manual  = mean(man_mean[s]),
    system_rmdcev     = rm_mean[J + 1],
    system_manual     = man_mean[J + 1],
    max_abs_pct_diff  = max(abs(cmp$pct_diff)),
    max_draw_diff     = max(cmp$max_draw_diff),
    entry_share       = mean(entry, na.rm = TRUE)
  )
 
  write.csv(cmp, paste0("welfare_compare_same_draws_", yr, ".csv"), row.names = FALSE)
  saveRDS(list(compare = cmp, overview = overview, rm_draws = rm_draws, man_draws = man_draws,
               theta_rows = rm_idx, entry = entry),
          paste0("welfare_compare_same_draws_", yr, ".rds"))
  invisible(list(compare = cmp, overview = overview))
}
 
## ---- Loop over years ------------------------------------------------------------------
years <- c(2018, 2019,2020,2021,2022,2023)                     # add years as their welfare_YYYY objects exist
cmp_all <- lapply(years, function(Y) tryCatch(compare_same_draws(Y),
                  error = function(e) { message("year ", Y, ": ", conditionMessage(e)); NULL }))

Fast Code Rum

This is from the last version sent from the KT script.

##############
# 2018 - RUM #
##############
library(dplyr); library(tidyr); library(dfidx); library(mlogit); library(MASS)

############################################
## 1. Data: 52 weekly choice occasions per camper
############################################
d     <- final_data_2018 %>% dplyr::select(id, parkname, VisitCount, price) %>% mutate(id = as.character(id))
sites <- sort(unique(d$parkname))

trips <- d %>% filter(VisitCount > 0) %>% uncount(VisitCount) %>%
  group_by(id) %>% mutate(week = row_number()) %>% ungroup() %>%
  dplyr::select(id, week, chosen = parkname)
stopifnot(max(trips$week) <= 52)

occ <- expand_grid(id = unique(d$id), week = 1:52) %>%
  left_join(trips, by = c("id", "week")) %>%
  mutate(chosen = replace_na(chosen, "Stay_Home"), occ_id = paste0(id, "_w", week))

long <- expand_grid(occ, alt = c(sites, "Stay_Home")) %>%
  left_join(d %>% dplyr::select(id, alt = parkname, price), by = c("id", "alt")) %>%
  mutate(price  = ifelse(alt == "Stay_Home", 0, price),
         choice = alt == chosen)
stopifnot(!anyNA(long$price))

dl <- dfidx(long, idx = list("occ_id", "alt"), shape = "long")

###########################################################################
## 2. Repeated RUM: travel cost + site constants, stay home as reference ##
###########################################################################
m <- mlogit(choice ~ price | 1, data = dl, reflevel = "Stay_Home")
summary(m)

############################################################################
## 3. Objects for welfare: one occasion per camper (all 52 are identical) ##
############################################################################
X    <- model.matrix(m)
oid  <- as.character(idx(dl, 1)); alt <- as.character(idx(dl, 2))
alts <- alt[1:length(unique(alt))]; K <- length(alts)
stopifnot(all(alt == rep(alts, length(unique(oid)))))
first <- grepl("_w1$", oid)
X1    <- X[first, , drop = FALSE]
pid   <- sub("_w1$", "", oid[first][seq(1, sum(first), by = K)])
site_cols <- match(sites, alts); home_col <- match("Stay_Home", alts)

N <- d %>% dplyr::select(id, parkname, VisitCount) %>%
  pivot_wider(names_from = parkname, values_from = VisitCount, values_fill = 0)
N <- as.matrix(N[match(pid, N$id), sites])                 # observed trips, campers x sites

probs <- function(b) {                                     # choice probabilities, campers x alternatives
  V <- matrix(X1 %*% b, ncol = K, byrow = TRUE)
  P <- exp(V - apply(V, 1, max)); P / rowSums(P)
}

summ_kr <- function(point, kr) {                           # mean + 95% Krinsky-Robb bounds
  data.frame(mean  = point,
             lower = apply(kr, 2, quantile, 0.025),
             upper = apply(kr, 2, quantile, 0.975))
}

## Krinsky-Robb draws (same draws for both measures)
set.seed(898989 + 2018)
draws <- mvrnorm(1000, coef(m), vcov(m))

###############################################################
## 4a. UNCONDITIONAL welfare (log-sum x 52), mean per camper ##
###############################################################
welfare_unc <- function(b) {
  P  <- probs(b); bp <- -b[["price"]]
  Pj <- P[, site_cols, drop = FALSE]; P0 <- P[, home_col]
  site <- 52 * log(1 - Pj) / bp
  c(setNames(colMeans(site), sites),
    "Sum of single-site losses" = mean(rowSums(site)),
    "All sites closed"          = mean(52 * log(P0) / bp),
    "Average site closure"      = mean(colMeans(site)))
}
kr_unc <- t(apply(draws, 1, welfare_unc))
unc    <- summ_kr(welfare_unc(coef(m)), kr_unc)
round(unc, 3)

############################################
## 4b. CONDITIONAL welfare (von Haefen, 2003), mean per camper
############################################
welfare_con <- function(b) {
  P  <- probs(b); bp <- -b[["price"]]
  Pj <- P[, site_cols, drop = FALSE]; P0 <- P[, home_col]; n <- rowSums(N)
  site <- N * log(1 - Pj) / (Pj * bp)
  c(setNames(colMeans(site), sites),
    "Sum of single-site losses" = mean(rowSums(site)),
    "All sites closed"          = mean(n * log(P0) / ((1 - P0) * bp)),
    "Average site closure"      = mean(colMeans(site)))
}
kr_con <- t(apply(draws, 1, welfare_con))
con    <- summ_kr(welfare_con(coef(m)), kr_con)
round(con, 3)

############################################
## 4c. UNCONDITIONAL welfare PER VISITOR: mean over the campers who visited each site
############################################
vis <- N > 0                                               # campers x sites: visited the site
nv  <- colSums(vis)                                        # number of visitors of each site

welfare_unc_vis <- function(b) {
  P  <- probs(b); bp <- -b[["price"]]
  Pj <- P[, site_cols, drop = FALSE]; P0 <- P[, home_col]
  site <- 52 * log(1 - Pj) / bp                            # as in 4a
  part <- rowSums(vis) > 0                                 # campers who visited at least one site
  w <- colSums(site * vis) / nv                            # mean over the campers who visited the site
  c(setNames(w, sites),
    "Sum of single-site losses" = mean(rowSums(site * vis)[part]),  # each camper's losses at the sites they visited
    "All sites closed"          = mean(52 * log(P0[part]) / bp),
    "Average site closure"      = mean(w))
}
kr_unc_vis <- t(apply(draws, 1, welfare_unc_vis))
unc_vis    <- summ_kr(welfare_unc_vis(coef(m)), kr_unc_vis)
round(unc_vis, 3)
                                        mean    lower    upper
Ainapo Cabin                         -16.683  -23.380  -12.261

############################################
## 5. Demand simulations: % change in total trips and elasticity
############################################
s_vec <- c(0.01, 0.10, 0.20, 0.50)

pred_total <- function(b, s) {
  prob <- function(Xm) {
    V <- matrix(Xm %*% b, ncol = K, byrow = TRUE)
    P <- exp(V - apply(V, 1, max)); P / rowSums(P)
  }
  Xn <- X1; Xn[, "price"] <- (1 + s) * X1[, "price"]   # Stay_Home price stays 0
  base <- 52 * sum(prob(X1)[, site_cols])
  new  <- 52 * sum(prob(Xn)[, site_cols])
  100 * (new / base - 1)                                 # % change in total trips
}

draws <- mvrnorm(1000, coef(m), vcov(m))                 # Krinsky–Robb

pct_pt <- sapply(s_vec, function(s) pred_total(coef(m), s))
pct_kr <- sapply(s_vec, function(s) apply(draws, 1, pred_total, s = s))   # 1000 x 4
el_kr  <- sweep(pct_kr, 2, 100 * s_vec, "/")

out <- rbind(pct_change = pct_pt,
             pct_2.5    = apply(pct_kr, 2, quantile, .025),
             pct_97.5   = apply(pct_kr, 2, quantile, .975),
             elasticity = pct_pt / (100 * s_vec),
             el_2.5     = apply(el_kr, 2, quantile, .025),
             el_97.5    = apply(el_kr, 2, quantile, .975))
colnames(out) <- paste0("+", 100 * s_vec, "%")
round(out, 3)

elasticity -0.884 -0.821  -0.760  -0.622
el_2.5     -0.902 -0.836  -0.773  -0.632
el_97.5    -0.865 -0.804  -0.745  -0.612

############################################################################
## 3. Objects for welfare: one occasion per camper (all 52 are identical) ##
############################################################################
X    <- model.matrix(m)
oid  <- as.character(idx(dl, 1)); alt <- as.character(idx(dl, 2))
alts <- alt[1:length(unique(alt))]; K <- length(alts)
stopifnot(all(alt == rep(alts, length(unique(oid)))))
first <- grepl("_w1$", oid)
X1    <- X[first, , drop = FALSE]
pid   <- sub("_w1$", "", oid[first][seq(1, sum(first), by = K)])
site_cols <- match(sites, alts); home_col <- match("Stay_Home", alts)

N <- d %>% dplyr::select(id, parkname, VisitCount) %>%
  pivot_wider(names_from = parkname, values_from = VisitCount, values_fill = 0)
N <- as.matrix(N[match(pid, N$id), sites])                 # observed trips, campers x sites

probs <- function(b) {                                     # choice probabilities, campers x alternatives
  V <- matrix(X1 %*% b, ncol = K, byrow = TRUE)
  P <- exp(V - apply(V, 1, max)); P / rowSums(P)
}

summ_kr <- function(point, kr) {                           # mean + 95% Krinsky-Robb bounds
  data.frame(mean  = point,
             lower = apply(kr, 2, quantile, 0.025),
             upper = apply(kr, 2, quantile, 0.975))
}

## Krinsky-Robb draws (same draws for both measures)
set.seed(898989 + 2019)
draws <- mvrnorm(1000, coef(m), vcov(m))

###############################################################
## 4a. UNCONDITIONAL welfare (log-sum x 52), mean per camper ##
###############################################################
welfare_unc <- function(b) {
  P  <- probs(b); bp <- -b[["price"]]
  Pj <- P[, site_cols, drop = FALSE]; P0 <- P[, home_col]
  site <- 52 * log(1 - Pj) / bp
  c(setNames(colMeans(site), sites),
    "Sum of single-site losses" = mean(rowSums(site)),
    "All sites closed"          = mean(52 * log(P0) / bp),
    "Average site closure"      = mean(colMeans(site)))
}
kr_unc <- t(apply(draws, 1, welfare_unc))
unc    <- summ_kr(welfare_unc(coef(m)), kr_unc)
round(unc, 3)

############################################
## 4b. CONDITIONAL welfare (von Haefen, 2003), mean per camper
############################################
welfare_con <- function(b) {
  P  <- probs(b); bp <- -b[["price"]]
  Pj <- P[, site_cols, drop = FALSE]; P0 <- P[, home_col]; n <- rowSums(N)
  site <- N * log(1 - Pj) / (Pj * bp)
  c(setNames(colMeans(site), sites),
    "Sum of single-site losses" = mean(rowSums(site)),
    "All sites closed"          = mean(n * log(P0) / ((1 - P0) * bp)),
    "Average site closure"      = mean(colMeans(site)))
}
kr_con <- t(apply(draws, 1, welfare_con))
con    <- summ_kr(welfare_con(coef(m)), kr_con)
round(con, 3)

############################################
## 4c. UNCONDITIONAL welfare PER VISITOR: mean over the campers who visited each site
############################################
vis <- N > 0                                               # campers x sites: visited the site
nv  <- colSums(vis)                                        # number of visitors of each site

welfare_unc_vis <- function(b) {
  P  <- probs(b); bp <- -b[["price"]]
  Pj <- P[, site_cols, drop = FALSE]; P0 <- P[, home_col]
  site <- 52 * log(1 - Pj) / bp                            # as in 4a
  part <- rowSums(vis) > 0                                 # campers who visited at least one site
  w <- colSums(site * vis) / nv                            # mean over the campers who visited the site
  c(setNames(w, sites),
    "Sum of single-site losses" = mean(rowSums(site * vis)[part]),  # each camper's losses at the sites they visited
    "All sites closed"          = mean(52 * log(P0[part]) / bp),
    "Average site closure"      = mean(w))
}
kr_unc_vis <- t(apply(draws, 1, welfare_unc_vis))
unc_vis    <- summ_kr(welfare_unc_vis(coef(m)), kr_unc_vis)
round(unc_vis, 3)

############################################################################
## 3. Objects for welfare: one occasion per camper (all 52 are identical) ##
############################################################################
X    <- model.matrix(m)
oid  <- as.character(idx(dl, 1)); alt <- as.character(idx(dl, 2))
alts <- alt[1:length(unique(alt))]; K <- length(alts)
stopifnot(all(alt == rep(alts, length(unique(oid)))))
first <- grepl("_w1$", oid)
X1    <- X[first, , drop = FALSE]
pid   <- sub("_w1$", "", oid[first][seq(1, sum(first), by = K)])
site_cols <- match(sites, alts); home_col <- match("Stay_Home", alts)

N <- d %>% dplyr::select(id, parkname, VisitCount) %>%
  pivot_wider(names_from = parkname, values_from = VisitCount, values_fill = 0)
N <- as.matrix(N[match(pid, N$id), sites])                 # observed trips, campers x sites

probs <- function(b) {                                     # choice probabilities, campers x alternatives
  V <- matrix(X1 %*% b, ncol = K, byrow = TRUE)
  P <- exp(V - apply(V, 1, max)); P / rowSums(P)
}

summ_kr <- function(point, kr) {                           # mean + 95% Krinsky-Robb bounds
  data.frame(mean  = point,
             lower = apply(kr, 2, quantile, 0.025),
             upper = apply(kr, 2, quantile, 0.975))
}

## Krinsky-Robb draws (same draws for both measures)
set.seed(898989 + 2020)
draws <- mvrnorm(1000, coef(m), vcov(m))

###############################################################
## 4a. UNCONDITIONAL welfare (log-sum x 52), mean per camper ##
###############################################################
welfare_unc <- function(b) {
  P  <- probs(b); bp <- -b[["price"]]
  Pj <- P[, site_cols, drop = FALSE]; P0 <- P[, home_col]
  site <- 52 * log(1 - Pj) / bp
  c(setNames(colMeans(site), sites),
    "Sum of single-site losses" = mean(rowSums(site)),
    "All sites closed"          = mean(52 * log(P0) / bp),
    "Average site closure"      = mean(colMeans(site)))
}
kr_unc <- t(apply(draws, 1, welfare_unc))
unc    <- summ_kr(welfare_unc(coef(m)), kr_unc)
round(unc, 3)

############################################
## 4b. CONDITIONAL welfare (von Haefen, 2003), mean per camper
############################################
welfare_con <- function(b) {
  P  <- probs(b); bp <- -b[["price"]]
  Pj <- P[, site_cols, drop = FALSE]; P0 <- P[, home_col]; n <- rowSums(N)
  site <- N * log(1 - Pj) / (Pj * bp)
  c(setNames(colMeans(site), sites),
    "Sum of single-site losses" = mean(rowSums(site)),
    "All sites closed"          = mean(n * log(P0) / ((1 - P0) * bp)),
    "Average site closure"      = mean(colMeans(site)))
}
kr_con <- t(apply(draws, 1, welfare_con))
con    <- summ_kr(welfare_con(coef(m)), kr_con)
round(con, 3)

############################################
## 4c. UNCONDITIONAL welfare PER VISITOR: mean over the campers who visited each site
############################################
vis <- N > 0                                               # campers x sites: visited the site
nv  <- colSums(vis)                                        # number of visitors of each site

welfare_unc_vis <- function(b) {
  P  <- probs(b); bp <- -b[["price"]]
  Pj <- P[, site_cols, drop = FALSE]; P0 <- P[, home_col]
  site <- 52 * log(1 - Pj) / bp                            # as in 4a
  part <- rowSums(vis) > 0                                 # campers who visited at least one site
  w <- colSums(site * vis) / nv                            # mean over the campers who visited the site
  c(setNames(w, sites),
    "Sum of single-site losses" = mean(rowSums(site * vis)[part]),  # each camper's losses at the sites they visited
    "All sites closed"          = mean(52 * log(P0[part]) / bp),
    "Average site closure"      = mean(w))
}
kr_unc_vis <- t(apply(draws, 1, welfare_unc_vis))
unc_vis    <- summ_kr(welfare_unc_vis(coef(m)), kr_unc_vis)
round(unc_vis, 3)

############################################
## 5. Demand simulations: % change in total trips and elasticity
############################################
s_vec <- c(0.01, 0.10, 0.20, 0.50)

pred_total <- function(b, s) {
  prob <- function(Xm) {
    V <- matrix(Xm %*% b, ncol = K, byrow = TRUE)
    P <- exp(V - apply(V, 1, max)); P / rowSums(P)
  }
  Xn <- X1; Xn[, "price"] <- (1 + s) * X1[, "price"]   # Stay_Home price stays 0
  base <- 52 * sum(prob(X1)[, site_cols])
  new  <- 52 * sum(prob(Xn)[, site_cols])
  100 * (new / base - 1)                                 # % change in total trips
}

draws <- mvrnorm(1000, coef(m), vcov(m))                 # Krinsky–Robb

pct_pt <- sapply(s_vec, function(s) pred_total(coef(m), s))
pct_kr <- sapply(s_vec, function(s) apply(draws, 1, pred_total, s = s))   # 1000 x 4
el_kr  <- sweep(pct_kr, 2, 100 * s_vec, "/")

out <- rbind(pct_change = pct_pt,
             pct_2.5    = apply(pct_kr, 2, quantile, .025),
             pct_97.5   = apply(pct_kr, 2, quantile, .975),
             elasticity = pct_pt / (100 * s_vec),
             el_2.5     = apply(el_kr, 2, quantile, .025),
             el_97.5    = apply(el_kr, 2, quantile, .975))
colnames(out) <- paste0("+", 100 * s_vec, "%")
round(out, 3)
              +1%   +10%    +20%    +50%
pct_change -0.890 -8.266 -15.318 -31.521
pct_2.5    -0.911 -8.446 -15.636 -32.154
pct_97.5   -0.867 -8.062 -14.959 -30.843
elasticity -0.890 -0.827  -0.766  -0.630
el_2.5     -0.911 -0.845  -0.782  -0.643
el_97.5    -0.867 -0.806  -0.748  -0.617
# keep 2020 results under their own names
m_2020   <- m
unc_2020 <- unc
con_2020 <- con
dem_2020 <- out
unc_vis_2020 <- unc_vis

## per-draw results for the stability tests
saveRDS(list(W = kr_unc, W_vis = kr_unc_vis, el = el_kr), "rum_draws_2020.rds")

  group_by(id) %>% mutate(week = row_number()) %>% ungroup() %>%
  dplyr::select(id, week, chosen = parkname)

  left_join(trips, by = c("id", "week")) %>%
  mutate(chosen = replace_na(chosen, "Stay_Home"), occ_id = paste0(id, "_w", week))

long <- expand_grid(occ, alt = c(sites, "Stay_Home")) %>%
  left_join(d %>% dplyr::select(id, alt = parkname, price), by = c("id", "alt")) %>%
  mutate(price  = ifelse(alt == "Stay_Home", 0, price),
         choice = alt == chosen)
stopifnot(!anyNA(long$price))

X    <- model.matrix(m)
oid  <- as.character(idx(dl, 1)); alt <- as.character(idx(dl, 2))
alts <- alt[1:length(unique(alt))]; K <- length(alts)
stopifnot(all(alt == rep(alts, length(unique(oid)))))
first <- grepl("_w1$", oid)
X1    <- X[first, , drop = FALSE]
pid   <- sub("_w1$", "", oid[first][seq(1, sum(first), by = K)])
site_cols <- match(sites, alts); home_col <- match("Stay_Home", alts)

N <- d %>% dplyr::select(id, parkname, VisitCount) %>%
  pivot_wider(names_from = parkname, values_from = VisitCount, values_fill = 0)
N <- as.matrix(N[match(pid, N$id), sites])                 # observed trips, campers x sites

probs <- function(b) {                                     # choice probabilities, campers x alternatives
  V <- matrix(X1 %*% b, ncol = K, byrow = TRUE)
  P <- exp(V - apply(V, 1, max)); P / rowSums(P)
}

summ_kr <- function(point, kr) {                           # mean + 95% Krinsky-Robb bounds
  data.frame(mean  = point,
             lower = apply(kr, 2, quantile, 0.025),
             upper = apply(kr, 2, quantile, 0.975))
}

## Krinsky-Robb draws (same draws for both measures)
set.seed(898989 + 2021)
draws <- mvrnorm(1000, coef(m), vcov(m))

###############################################################
## 4a. UNCONDITIONAL welfare (log-sum x 52), mean per camper ##
###############################################################
welfare_unc <- function(b) {
  P  <- probs(b); bp <- -b[["price"]]
  Pj <- P[, site_cols, drop = FALSE]; P0 <- P[, home_col]
  site <- 52 * log(1 - Pj) / bp
  c(setNames(colMeans(site), sites),
    "Sum of single-site losses" = mean(rowSums(site)),
    "All sites closed"          = mean(52 * log(P0) / bp),
    "Average site closure"      = mean(colMeans(site)))
}
kr_unc <- t(apply(draws, 1, welfare_unc))
unc    <- summ_kr(welfare_unc(coef(m)), kr_unc)
round(unc, 3)

############################################
## 4b. CONDITIONAL welfare (von Haefen, 2003), mean per camper
############################################
welfare_con <- function(b) {
  P  <- probs(b); bp <- -b[["price"]]
  Pj <- P[, site_cols, drop = FALSE]; P0 <- P[, home_col]; n <- rowSums(N)
  site <- N * log(1 - Pj) / (Pj * bp)
  c(setNames(colMeans(site), sites),
    "Sum of single-site losses" = mean(rowSums(site)),
    "All sites closed"          = mean(n * log(P0) / ((1 - P0) * bp)),
    "Average site closure"      = mean(colMeans(site)))
}
kr_con <- t(apply(draws, 1, welfare_con))
con    <- summ_kr(welfare_con(coef(m)), kr_con)
round(con, 3)

############################################
## 4c. UNCONDITIONAL welfare PER VISITOR: mean over the campers who visited each site
############################################
vis <- N > 0                                               # campers x sites: visited the site
nv  <- colSums(vis)                                        # number of visitors of each site

welfare_unc_vis <- function(b) {
  P  <- probs(b); bp <- -b[["price"]]
  Pj <- P[, site_cols, drop = FALSE]; P0 <- P[, home_col]
  site <- 52 * log(1 - Pj) / bp                            # as in 4a
  part <- rowSums(vis) > 0                                 # campers who visited at least one site
  w <- colSums(site * vis) / nv                            # mean over the campers who visited the site
  c(setNames(w, sites),
    "Sum of single-site losses" = mean(rowSums(site * vis)[part]),  # each camper's losses at the sites they visited
    "All sites closed"          = mean(52 * log(P0[part]) / bp),
    "Average site closure"      = mean(w))
}
kr_unc_vis <- t(apply(draws, 1, welfare_unc_vis))
unc_vis    <- summ_kr(welfare_unc_vis(coef(m)), kr_unc_vis)
round(unc_vis, 3)

############################################
## 5. Demand simulations: % change in total trips and elasticity
############################################
s_vec <- c(0.01, 0.10, 0.20, 0.50)

pred_total <- function(b, s) {
  prob <- function(Xm) {
    V <- matrix(Xm %*% b, ncol = K, byrow = TRUE)
    P <- exp(V - apply(V, 1, max)); P / rowSums(P)
  }
  Xn <- X1; Xn[, "price"] <- (1 + s) * X1[, "price"]   # Stay_Home price stays 0
  base <- 52 * sum(prob(X1)[, site_cols])
  new  <- 52 * sum(prob(Xn)[, site_cols])
  100 * (new / base - 1)                                 # % change in total trips
}

draws <- mvrnorm(1000, coef(m), vcov(m))                 # Krinsky–Robb

pct_pt <- sapply(s_vec, function(s) pred_total(coef(m), s))
pct_kr <- sapply(s_vec, function(s) apply(draws, 1, pred_total, s = s))   # 1000 x 4
el_kr  <- sweep(pct_kr, 2, 100 * s_vec, "/")

out <- rbind(pct_change = pct_pt,
             pct_2.5    = apply(pct_kr, 2, quantile, .025),
             pct_97.5   = apply(pct_kr, 2, quantile, .975),
             elasticity = pct_pt / (100 * s_vec),
             el_2.5     = apply(el_kr, 2, quantile, .025),
             el_97.5    = apply(el_kr, 2, quantile, .975))
colnames(out) <- paste0("+", 100 * s_vec, "%")
round(out, 3)
              +1%   +10%    +20%    +50%
pct_change -0.922 -8.547 -15.831 -32.582
pct_2.5    -0.940 -8.712 -16.118 -33.103
pct_97.5   -0.903 -8.387 -15.550 -32.036
elasticity -0.922 -0.855  -0.792  -0.652
el_2.5     -0.940 -0.871  -0.806  -0.662
el_97.5    -0.903 -0.839  -0.777  -0.641
# keep 2021 results under their own names
m_2021   <- m
unc_2021 <- unc
con_2021 <- con
dem_2021 <- out
unc_vis_2021 <- unc_vis

## per-draw results for the stability tests
saveRDS(list(W = kr_unc, W_vis = kr_unc_vis, el = el_kr), "rum_draws_2021.rds")

trips <- d %>% filter(VisitCount > 0) %>% uncount(VisitCount) %>%
  group_by(id) %>% mutate(week = row_number()) %>% ungroup() %>%
  dplyr::select(id, week, chosen = parkname)
stopifnot(max(trips$week) <= 52)

occ <- expand_grid(id = unique(d$id), week = 1:52) %>%
  left_join(trips, by = c("id", "week")) %>%
  mutate(chosen = replace_na(chosen, "Stay_Home"), occ_id = paste0(id, "_w", week))

long <- expand_grid(occ, alt = c(sites, "Stay_Home")) %>%
  left_join(d %>% dplyr::select(id, alt = parkname, price), by = c("id", "alt")) %>%
  mutate(price  = ifelse(alt == "Stay_Home", 0, price),
         choice = alt == chosen)
stopifnot(!anyNA(long$price))

dl <- dfidx(long, idx = list("occ_id", "alt"), shape = "long")

############################################
## 4c. UNCONDITIONAL welfare PER VISITOR: mean over the campers who visited each site
############################################
vis <- N > 0                                               # campers x sites: visited the site
nv  <- colSums(vis)                                        # number of visitors of each site

welfare_unc_vis <- function(b) {
  P  <- probs(b); bp <- -b[["price"]]
  Pj <- P[, site_cols, drop = FALSE]; P0 <- P[, home_col]
  site <- 52 * log(1 - Pj) / bp                            # as in 4a
  part <- rowSums(vis) > 0                                 # campers who visited at least one site
  w <- colSums(site * vis) / nv                            # mean over the campers who visited the site
  c(setNames(w, sites),
    "Sum of single-site losses" = mean(rowSums(site * vis)[part]),  # each camper's losses at the sites they visited
    "All sites closed"          = mean(52 * log(P0[part]) / bp),
    "Average site closure"      = mean(w))
}

############################################
## 5. Demand simulations: % change in total trips and elasticity
############################################
s_vec <- c(0.01, 0.10, 0.20, 0.50)

pred_total <- function(b, s) {
  prob <- function(Xm) {
    V <- matrix(Xm %*% b, ncol = K, byrow = TRUE)
    P <- exp(V - apply(V, 1, max)); P / rowSums(P)
  }
  Xn <- X1; Xn[, "price"] <- (1 + s) * X1[, "price"]   # Stay_Home price stays 0
  base <- 52 * sum(prob(X1)[, site_cols])
  new  <- 52 * sum(prob(Xn)[, site_cols])
  100 * (new / base - 1)                                 # % change in total trips
}

draws <- mvrnorm(1000, coef(m), vcov(m))                 # Krinsky–Robb

pct_pt <- sapply(s_vec, function(s) pred_total(coef(m), s))
pct_kr <- sapply(s_vec, function(s) apply(draws, 1, pred_total, s = s))   # 1000 x 4
el_kr  <- sweep(pct_kr, 2, 100 * s_vec, "/")

out <- rbind(pct_change = pct_pt,
             pct_2.5    = apply(pct_kr, 2, quantile, .025),
             pct_97.5   = apply(pct_kr, 2, quantile, .975),
             elasticity = pct_pt / (100 * s_vec),
             el_2.5     = apply(el_kr, 2, quantile, .025),
             el_97.5    = apply(el_kr, 2, quantile, .975))
colnames(out) <- paste0("+", 100 * s_vec, "%")
round(out, 3)
              +1%   +10%    +20%    +50%

elasticity -0.915 -0.849  -0.787  -0.649
el_2.5     -0.934 -0.867  -0.803  -0.661
el_97.5    -0.895 -0.832  -0.772  -0.637

unc_2022 <- unc

############################################################################
## 3. Objects for welfare: one occasion per camper (all 52 are identical) ##
############################################################################
X    <- model.matrix(m)
oid  <- as.character(idx(dl, 1)); alt <- as.character(idx(dl, 2))
alts <- alt[1:length(unique(alt))]; K <- length(alts)
stopifnot(all(alt == rep(alts, length(unique(oid)))))
first <- grepl("_w1$", oid)
X1    <- X[first, , drop = FALSE]
pid   <- sub("_w1$", "", oid[first][seq(1, sum(first), by = K)])
site_cols <- match(sites, alts); home_col <- match("Stay_Home", alts)

N <- d %>% dplyr::select(id, parkname, VisitCount) %>%
  pivot_wider(names_from = parkname, values_from = VisitCount, values_fill = 0)
N <- as.matrix(N[match(pid, N$id), sites])                 # observed trips, campers x sites

probs <- function(b) {                                     # choice probabilities, campers x alternatives
  V <- matrix(X1 %*% b, ncol = K, byrow = TRUE)
  P <- exp(V - apply(V, 1, max)); P / rowSums(P)
}

summ_kr <- function(point, kr) {                           # mean + 95% Krinsky-Robb bounds
  data.frame(mean  = point,
             lower = apply(kr, 2, quantile, 0.025),
             upper = apply(kr, 2, quantile, 0.975))
}

## Krinsky-Robb draws (same draws for both measures)
set.seed(898989 + 2023)
draws <- mvrnorm(1000, coef(m), vcov(m))

###############################################################
## 4a. UNCONDITIONAL welfare (log-sum x 52), mean per camper ##
###############################################################
welfare_unc <- function(b) {
  P  <- probs(b); bp <- -b[["price"]]
  Pj <- P[, site_cols, drop = FALSE]; P0 <- P[, home_col]
  site <- 52 * log(1 - Pj) / bp
  c(setNames(colMeans(site), sites),
    "Sum of single-site losses" = mean(rowSums(site)),
    "All sites closed"          = mean(52 * log(P0) / bp),
    "Average site closure"      = mean(colMeans(site)))
}
kr_unc <- t(apply(draws, 1, welfare_unc))
unc    <- summ_kr(welfare_unc(coef(m)), kr_unc)
round(unc, 3)

############################################
## 4b. CONDITIONAL welfare (von Haefen, 2003), mean per camper
############################################
welfare_con <- function(b) {
  P  <- probs(b); bp <- -b[["price"]]
  Pj <- P[, site_cols, drop = FALSE]; P0 <- P[, home_col]; n <- rowSums(N)
  site <- N * log(1 - Pj) / (Pj * bp)
  c(setNames(colMeans(site), sites),
    "Sum of single-site losses" = mean(rowSums(site)),
    "All sites closed"          = mean(n * log(P0) / ((1 - P0) * bp)),
    "Average site closure"      = mean(colMeans(site)))
}
kr_con <- t(apply(draws, 1, welfare_con))
con    <- summ_kr(welfare_con(coef(m)), kr_con)
round(con, 3)

Comparision

This runs the fast and the Original and compares the two. Note that

suppressMessages({ library(mlogit); library(dfidx) })

## ---- helpers ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
find_mlogit <- function(obj) {                       # first mlogit object inside obj
    if (inherits(obj, "mlogit")) return(obj)
    if (is.list(obj) && !is.data.frame(obj))
        for (el in obj) { f <- find_mlogit(el); if (!is.null(f)) return(f) }
    NULL
}

## unconditional welfare (point estimate) straight from an mlogit model.
## Every camper has the same 52 occasions, so the mean over occasions = the mean per camper.
## The reference ("stay home") alternative is the one without an intercept, and the price
## coefficient is the one coefficient that is not an intercept, so names need not match.
rum_unc <- function(mod) {
    b  <- coef(mod); X <- model.matrix(mod)
    ix <- tryCatch(list(idx(mod, 1), idx(mod, 2)),
                   error = function(e) list(idx(mod$model, 1), idx(mod$model, 2)))
    oid <- as.character(ix[[1]]); alt <- as.character(ix[[2]])
    alts <- unique(alt); K <- length(alts); nocc <- length(unique(oid))
    stopifnot(length(alt) == K * nocc, all(alt == rep(alts, nocc)))
    
    int_alts <- sub("^\\(Intercept\\):", "", grep("^\\(Intercept\\):", names(b), value = TRUE))
    home     <- setdiff(alts, int_alts); stopifnot(length(home) == 1)
    pname    <- setdiff(names(b), grep("^\\(Intercept\\):", names(b), value = TRUE))
    stopifnot(length(pname) == 1)                      # travel cost only
    sites    <- sort(setdiff(alts, home))
    
    V  <- matrix(X %*% b[colnames(X)], ncol = K, byrow = TRUE)
    P  <- exp(V - apply(V, 1, max)); P <- P / rowSums(P)
    bp <- -b[[pname]]
    site <- 52 * log(1 - P[, match(sites, alts), drop = FALSE]) / bp
    list(welfare = c(setNames(colMeans(site), sites),
                     "Sum of single-site losses" = mean(rowSums(site)),
                     "All sites closed"          = mean(52 * log(P[, match(home, alts)]) / bp),
                     "Average site closure"      = mean(colMeans(site))),
         info = c(occasions = nocc, campers = nocc / 52, alternatives = K,
                  logLik = as.numeric(logLik(mod)), price = b[[pname]]),
         home = home, price_name = pname)
}

## ---- comparison for one year ------------------------------------------------------------------
compare_rum_year <- function(YEAR) {
    yr  <- as.character(YEAR)
    new_m   <- get(paste0("m_", yr))
    new_unc <- get(paste0("unc_", yr))
    old_m   <- find_mlogit(model_list_52[[paste0("52wk ", yr)]])
    if (is.null(old_m)) stop("no mlogit model found in model_list_52$`52wk ", yr, "`")
    
    new_r <- rum_unc(new_m); old_r <- rum_unc(old_m)
    
    ## self-check: the helper reproduces the script's own unconditional welfare
    chk <- max(abs(new_r$welfare - new_unc[names(new_r$welfare), "mean"]))
    cat("\n=====", yr, "=====\nself-check (helper vs unc_", yr, "): max |diff| = ",
        signif(chk, 3), "\n", sep = "")
    
    ## model information
    info <- rbind(new = new_r$info, old = old_r$info)
    print(round(info, 4))
    if (new_r$home != old_r$home || new_r$price_name != old_r$price_name)
        cat("note: reference / price names differ (new:", new_r$home, new_r$price_name,
            " old:", old_r$home, old_r$price_name, ")\n")
    
    ## coefficients, matched by name
    bn <- coef(new_m); bo <- coef(old_m)
    cn <- union(names(bn), names(bo))
    coefs <- data.frame(coef = cn, new = unname(bn[cn]), old = unname(bo[cn]))
    coefs$diff <- coefs$new - coefs$old
    cat("coefficients: max |diff| =", signif(max(abs(coefs$diff), na.rm = TRUE), 3),
        "; only in one model:", sum(is.na(coefs$diff)), "\n")
    
    ## unconditional welfare, matched by measure name
    mn <- union(names(new_r$welfare), names(old_r$welfare))
    wel <- data.frame(year = YEAR, measure = mn,
                      new = unname(new_r$welfare[mn]), old = unname(old_r$welfare[mn]))
    wel$diff     <- wel$new - wel$old
    wel$pct_diff <- 100 * wel$diff / abs(wel$old)
    print(wel[, -1], digits = 4, row.names = FALSE)
    
    overview <- data.frame(
        year              = YEAR,
        same_campers      = info["new", "campers"] == info["old", "campers"],
        same_alternatives = info["new", "alternatives"] == info["old", "alternatives"],
        logLik_new        = info["new", "logLik"],
        logLik_old        = info["old", "logLik"],
        max_abs_coef_diff = max(abs(coefs$diff), na.rm = TRUE),
        coefs_unmatched   = sum(is.na(coefs$diff)),
        all_closed_new    = new_r$welfare[["All sites closed"]],
        all_closed_old    = old_r$welfare[["All sites closed"]],
        max_abs_pct_welf  = max(abs(wel$pct_diff), na.rm = TRUE),
        measures_unmatched = sum(is.na(wel$diff))
    )
    invisible(list(coefs = coefs, welfare = wel, overview = overview))
}

## ---- run all years ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
print(names(model_list_52))                          # check the element names first
[1] "52wk 2018" "52wk 2019" "52wk 2020" "52wk 2021" "52wk 2022" "52wk 2023"
years <- 2018:2023
rum_cmp <- lapply(years, function(Y) tryCatch(compare_rum_year(Y),
                                              error = function(e) { message("year ", Y, ": ", conditionMessage(e)); NULL }))
